### [33] [DynaResize: Runtime GPU Reallocation for Disaggregated LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22614)
*Hanlin Du,Zhiyuan Yan,Haiquan Chen,Jiarui Fang,Yungang Bao,Sa wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DynaResize是一个运行时GPU重分配系统，通过动态切换Rollout和Training之间的GPU来平衡阶段执行时间，在不改变RL语义的前提下，相比最优静态配置提升66.5%端到端吞吐量、减少33%执行时间，并隐藏27%角色切换开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RL的大模型后训练中，Rollout和Training被分散到独立GPU资源上，但静态GPU分区在长尾Rollout延迟下会产生严重的流水线气泡，导致资源利用率低下。

Method: DynaResize将GPU重分配分解为细粒度操作，通过通信器复用（communicator reuse）、有界状态暂存（bounded state staging）和基于滞后的重分配（hysteresis-based resizing）将非启动关键工作从关键路径中移除。

Result: 端到端吞吐量提升66.5%，总执行时间减少33%，隐藏27%的角色切换开销。

Conclusion: DynaResize通过动态GPU重分配有效解决了静态分区下的流水线气泡问题，显著提升了RL后训练的效率，且不改变强化学习语义。

Abstract: RL-based LLM post-training increasingly disaggregates Rollout and Training across separate GPU resources, but static GPU partitioning suffers from severe pipeline bubbles under long-tail rollout latency. We present DynaResize, a runtime GPU reallocation system that dynamically switches GPUs between Rollout and Training to balance stage execution times without changing RL semantics. DynaResize decomposes resizing into fine-grained operations and removes non-startup-critical work from the critical path through communicator reuse, bounded state staging, and hysteresis-based resizing. Experimental results show that DynaResize can improve end-to-end throughput by 66.5% and reduce total execution time by 33% over the optimal static configuration, while hiding 27% of role-switching overhead.

</details>


### [34] [VlogReward: Learning Multi-Dimensional Evaluation for Vlog Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22632)
*Yexiang Liu,Wen Zhong,Sijie Zhu,Xin Gu,Fan Chen,Junxian Duan,Jie Cao,Longyin Wen,Zhenfang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对vlog评估缺乏标准化标准、数据集和奖励模型的问题，提出了VlogReward奖励模型。研究首先定义了包含六维度的评估框架，构建了10万条vlog编辑数据集和VRMBench基准，然后利用改进的GRPO框架（引入可调组间比较奖励）训练VlogReward模型，该模型在细粒度评分和可操作反馈方面显著优于GPT-5和Gemini-3-Pro等现有MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vlog作为个性化叙事媒介的兴起需要自动化评估系统，但vlog评估具有高度主观性，缺乏标准化标准、数据集、基准和有效的奖励模型。

Method: 1) 联合专业vlog创作者和产品经理定义六维评估框架（Creativity, Consistency, Concept Design, Cinematography, Narration, Pacing）；2) 构建10万条vlog编辑数据集和VRMBench基准；3) 提出VlogReward模型，在GRPO框架中引入可调组间比较奖励，解决标准GRPO的"方向盲视"问题。

Result: VlogReward在细粒度多维评分和可操作反馈方面达到SOTA，显著优于GPT-5和Gemini-3-Pro等现有MLLM。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于vlog创作者并推动自动化vlog评估和优化系统的发展。

Abstract: The rapid rise of vlogs as a personalized storytelling medium has created a demand for automated systems to evaluate and refine vlog editing plans. However, vlog assessment is highly subjective and remains challenging due to a lack of standardized criteria, dataset and benchmark, and effective reward models. To address these challenges, we define a comprehensive vlog evaluation framework guided by professional vlog creators and product managers, establishing a taxonomy of six key dimensions, i.e., Creativity, Consistency, Concept Design, Cinematography, Narration, and Pacing. Subsequently, we curate a large-scale dataset of 100k vlog edits and a dedicated benchmark, VRMBench, to evaluate the vlog rewarding capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Finally, we present VlogReward, a robust vlog reward model that can provide both fine-grained multi-dimensional scores and actionable feedback for iterative refinement. Technically, we enhance the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) framework by introducing an adjustable inter-group comparison reward, which mitigates the "direction blindness" issue of standard GRPO and enables the model to better distinguish varied-quality edits. VlogReward achieves state-of-the-art results that significantly outperform existing MLLMs, including GPT-5 and Gemini-3-Pro. We hope that our study can help vlog creators and foster automated vlog evaluation and refinement systems.

</details>


### [35] [Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous Sensor Selection in Maritime Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22667)
*Andrei Starodubov,Yaqub Aris Prabowo,Andreas Hadjipieris,Roberto Galeazzi,Ioannis Kyriakides*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息增益引导的强化学习传感器选择框架，用于异构海事传感器网络中的单船跟踪。通过PPO智能体在每个决策时刻仅激活一个传感器，在保持接近全传感器跟踪性能的同时，显著降低了计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法要么同时激活所有传感器造成资源浪费，要么反复执行计算昂贵的在线期望信息增益评估，缺乏高效且可学习的传感器选择策略。

Method: 采用贝叶斯序贯蒙特卡洛跟踪器估计船只状态并提供信念表征；PPO智能体基于信念状态、检测历史、覆盖范围、传感器几何和已实现信息增益特征进行决策；奖励定义为由可观测性掩码门控的已实现信息增益项。

Result: 在塞浦路斯阿依纳帕码头的CMMI智能码头仿真环境中，学习策略的跟踪性能接近全传感器同时激活模式，但每个时刻仅激活一个传感器，且避免了期望信息增益方法所需的计算密集的在线熵搜索。

Conclusion: 所提出的信息增益引导强化学习框架能够在不牺牲跟踪精度的前提下实现高效的传感器调度，适用于非线性非高斯条件下的异构海事传感器网络。

Abstract: This paper presents an information-gain-guided reinforcement-learning sensor-selection framework for single-vessel tracking in heterogeneous maritime sensor networks. The proposed approach is motivated by information-theoretic sensor management: instead of activating all sensors or repeatedly performing computationally expensive online expected-information-gain evaluation, a learned policy selects one tracking-relevant sensor at each decision epoch. A Bayesian sequential Monte Carlo tracker estimates the vessel state from noisy measurements and provides a belief representation for scheduling under nonlinear and non-Gaussian conditions. A Proximal Policy Optimization agent selects one of five sensors deployed in a georeferenced simulation of the CMMI Smart Marina testbed at Ayia Napa Marina, Cyprus. The agent observes belief-state, detection-history, coverage, sensor-geometry, and realized-information-gain features. The reward is defined as a realized-information-gain term gated by an observability mask. Final-test simulations compare the proposed framework with random single-sensor selection, always-on sensing using all sensors simultaneously, and the expected-information-gain sensor-selection baseline proposed in our previous work. Results show that the learned policy achieves tracking performance close to always-on sensing while activating only one sensor per decision time step and avoiding the computationally expensive online entropy search required by expected-information-gain selection.

</details>


### [36] [Physical AI Governance: From Theory to Practice Across Life Cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22877)
*Wang Yang,Shaobo Wang,Hongxuan Liu,Xiaoran Cai,Yunyu He,Jingzong Zhou,Mengzhong Ma,Yi Yu,Rohit Sharma,Jingjing Fu,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了Physical AI（物理人工智能）的治理问题，针对其区别于传统AI的实时安全、动态环境交互和人机共存等特性，提出了一套涵盖研究、设计、数据、模型开发和部署五阶段的统一治理框架，将治理原则与工程实践相结合，为构建安全、可信且符合社会价值的Physical AI系统提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI治理框架无法有效应对Physical AI在实时安全约束、动态环境交互和人机共存等方面带来的独特治理挑战，亟需一个专门的治理框架来保障Physical AI系统的安全性和可信性。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，综合现有治理原则并将其组织为统一治理框架，进而提出Physical AI的五阶段生命周期（研究、设计、数据、模型开发、部署），并在每个阶段通过具体实践方法展示如何将治理落地实施。

Result: 形成了一个面向Physical AI的统一治理框架，并给出了各生命周期阶段可操作的治理实践方案。

Conclusion: 通过连接治理原则与工程工作流，为研究人员、开发者和政策制定者构建安全、可信且符合社会价值的Physical AI系统提供了结构化参考。

Abstract: With the emergence of Physical AI, artificial intelligence is extending beyond screen-based applications to embodied systems that perceive, interact with, and act in the physical world. Unlike traditional AI, Physical AI operates under real-time safety constraints, continuously interacts with dynamic environments, and coexists with humans, introducing governance challenges that existing AI governance frameworks do not explicitly address. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of Physical AI governance from both scientific and operational perspectives. We synthesize existing governance principles and organize them into a unified governance framework tailored to physical AI systems. Building on this foundation, we propose a five-stage Physical AI lifecycle comprising research, design, data, model development, and deployment, and demonstrate how governance can be operationalized across each stage through concrete implementation practices. By connecting governance principles with engineering workflows, this survey provides a structured reference for researchers, developers, and policymakers to build Physical AI systems that are safe, trustworthy, and aligned with societal values.

</details>


### [37] [Stress-testing large language model agents in a robotic chemistry laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23045)
*Lulu Guo,Yingkai Sun,Xiaobo Li,Luyao Ge,Ziming Wang,Haitao Zheng,Jingyu Li,Huijuan Zhang,Bingxu Chen,Daobin Liu,Yuebo Liu,Jie Li,Xiaohui Li,Linjiang Chen,Yi Luo,Jun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过机器人化学实验室的45个模块化工作站进行4608次试验，评估AI在物理世界中的科学 agency 能力。结果显示仅3.3%的工作流可执行，最佳系统达28.1%；长时程规划困难（仅3个工作流超过30步）；实验反馈仅引发局部调整而无全局重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估侧重于知识、推理和计划生成，但科学 agency 需要可靠的物理行动和基于证据的适应能力，目前缺乏对此的量化评估方法。

Method: 使用机器人化学实验室作为物理世界测试平台，将45个模块化工作站暴露为机器可读技能，进行4608次试验，由专家评估工作流的可执行性。

Result: 仅3.3%的试验产生可执行工作流；最佳系统达28.1%；仅3个可执行工作流超过30个操作（最长44个）；实验反馈仅引发局部调整，无工作流级重规划或分析方法重新设计。

Conclusion: 通过使物理可执行性和证据驱动重规划可测量，本研究提供了AI部署就绪性的基于证据的评估，以及指导闭环改进的诊断框架，以迈向物理扎根的自主研究。

Abstract: AI is evaluated through knowledge, reasoning and plan generation, yet scientific agency requires reliable physical action and adaptation to evidence. Here, we use a robotic chemistry laboratory as a physical-world testbed to make scientific agency measurable. Its 45 modular workstations exposed as machine-readable skills enabled 4,608 trials. Only 3.3% of trials produced expert-assessed executable workflows under laboratory constraints; even the best system achieved 28.1%. Long-horizon planning remained a challenge: only three executable workflows exceeded 30 operations, although the longest contained 44. Across five rounds, experimental feedback prompted local adjustments but no workflow-level replanning or analytical-method redesign. By making physical executability and evidence-driven replanning measurable, our study provides an evidence-based assessment of deployment readiness and a diagnostic framework to guide closed-loop improvements towards physically grounded autonomous research.

</details>


### [38] [Cost-Aware Recovery-Pathway Identification and Bayesian Optimization for Autonomous Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23896)
*Debajyoti Ray,Niranjan Srinivas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Coactive Learning方法，将自主实验室的实验决策建模为离散路径识别与连续路径内优化的序列决策问题，结合成本敏感贝叶斯假设判别（EC2）与高斯过程贝叶斯优化，在固定预算下实现路径识别成本与优化预算的平衡，在合成基准和CICERO选择性沉淀研究上表现接近oracle方法，优于提前承诺单一路径的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室不仅能自动化执行实验，还需决定哪条回收路径值得优化。现有方法缺乏对离散路径选择与连续参数优化联合决策的系统性处理，尤其在异构实验成本下如何平衡路径识别与路径内优化的预算分配尚待解决。

Method: 将问题形式化为两阶段序列决策：离散路径识别阶段（成本敏感贝叶斯假设判别，受EC2启发）和连续路径内优化阶段（高斯过程贝叶斯优化）。在明确假设下，固定预算单次尝试的期望花费受路径识别期望成本与路径内优化预算上限之和约束。

Result: 在合成基准和PNNL的CICERO选择性沉淀研究上的评估表明，该方法表现与oracle路径贝叶斯优化参考及强split-plate基线相当，且无需oracle提供正确路径标签。在NdFeB启发实例中，成功避免了提前承诺劣质氢氧化物路径的模拟惩罚。

Conclusion: Coactive Learning能有效平衡路径识别与路径内优化的预算分配，避免过早承诺次优路径，在异构实验成本场景下具有良好的鲁棒性，结论对成本模型假设的敏感性已做表征。

Abstract: Autonomous laboratories automate experimental execution, but a campaign must also decide which recovery pathway merits optimization. We formulate this as a sequential decision problem with a discrete pathway-identification stage and a continuous within-pathway optimization stage under heterogeneous experimental costs. Our implementation, Coactive learning, combines a cost-sensitive Bayesian hypothesis-discrimination policy motivated by EC2 (Golovin et al., 2010) with Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (Srinivas et al., 2010). Under explicitly stated assumptions, the expected spend of one fixed-budget campaign attempt is bounded by the expected pathway-identification cost plus the capped within-pathway optimization budget. We evaluate the method on synthetic benchmarks constrained by selected results reported for PNNL's CICERO selective-precipitation study (Ritchhart et al., 2026). The method performs comparably to an oracle-pathway Bayesian-optimization reference and to a strong split-plate baseline that discriminates pathways with its first plate, without receiving an oracle label for the correct pathway. It is given a candidate hypothesis space and a diagnostic likelihood model. On an NdFeB-inspired instance, it avoids the simulated penalty of a commit-first baseline that initially selects a plausible but inferior hydroxide pathway. This hypothetical wrong-first-commitment scenario is motivated by the hydroxide-oxalate performance contrast reported by CICERO. We characterize the sensitivity of these conclusions to the assumed cost model. The code and benchmark are open source.

</details>


### [39] [CRAFT: Learn the Schema, Execute the Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22642)
*Aakash Kolekar,Sahika Genc,Shahriar Shariat,Bunyamin Sisman,Tibor Mezi,Barbara Poblete,Shree Vandana Kachroo,Calvin Chi,Parth Parmar,Ari Singer,Prayaas Jain,Cindy Barker,Benoit Dumoulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRAFT是一种两阶段后训练方案，通过PLAN监督微调和执行形状强化学习，使编码智能体在不注入完整模式文档的情况下，掌握模式知识和工具使用行为，在广告分析场景中相比基线提升Agent Score 9.6个百分点，同时减少约9倍输入token负担和最多5倍模式发现循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业编码智能体将完整模式和工具文档注入每个提示词的做法，导致推理开销大、模式演化困难、多轮分析可靠性下降。研究能否通过后训练让智能体获取稳定的模式知识和工具使用行为，而非依赖提示词注入。

Method: CRAFT两阶段后训练方案：第一阶段，模式剥离的PLAN监督微调，从已验证轨迹中学习领域结构化计划和可执行行为，无需穷尽式提示时模式注入。第二阶段，执行形状强化学习，对齐工具选择、代码质量、计划-代码一致性和执行失败恢复的策略。训练轨迹通过Tri-Gate过滤器（执行验证、数据完整性检查、LLM评判推理审计）筛选。在广告分析场景评估，涵盖25个模式关联核心实体和30个智能体工作流。

Result: 相比模式填充基线，CRAFT的复合Agent Score提升9.6个百分点，一致性提升4.1个百分点，多轮连贯性提升4.2个百分点，输入token负担减少约9倍，模式发现循环减少最多5倍。同时报告了部署权衡、奖励塑形限制以及多轮工具使用强化学习所需的训练基础设施扩展。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过后训练方式有效实现了模式知识的内化，减少了推理时对模式文档注入的依赖，在提升性能的同时大幅降低推理成本，为生产级企业分析场景提供了可行的部署方案。

Abstract: Enterprise coding agents translate natural-language analytical requests into executable code over proprietary APIs, schemas, and metric definitions. Yet the prevailing deployment pattern injecting exhaustive schema and tool documentation into each prompt increases inference overhead, complicates schema evolution, and undermines reliability in multi-turn analysis. We investigate whether stable schema knowledge and tool-use behavior can instead be acquired through post-training while preserving the consistency required for production-facing analytics. We present CRAFT, a two-stage post-training recipe for schema-grounded coding agents. First, schema-stripped PLAN supervised fine-tuning learns domain-structured plans and executable behaviors from validated trajectories without exhaustive prompt-time schema injection. Second, execution-shaped reinforcement learning aligns the policy for tool selection, code quality, plan-code consistency, and recovery from failed executions. Training trajectories are curated through a Tri-Gate filter combining execution validation, data-integrity checks, and LLM-judge reasoning audit. We evaluate CRAFT for planned rollout in advertising analytics, covering campaign performance analysis, metric drill-downs, entity-level performance analysis, and multi-turn analytical refinement. The enterprise evaluation environment incorporates beta APIs as the agent-facing tool surface and spans 25 schema-linked core entities and 30 agentic workflows. Relative to a schema-stuffed baseline, CRAFT improves composite Agent Score by +9.6 pp, consistency by +4.1 pp, and multi-turn coherence by +4.2 pp, while reducing input-token burden by approximately 9x and schema-discovery loops by up to 5x. We further report deployment tradeoffs, reward-shaping limitations, and training-infrastructure extensions required for multi-turn tool-use reinforcement learning in enterprise settings.

</details>


### [40] [Reason Before You Retrieve: Agentic Planning for Multi-modal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22643)
*Tianyu Yang,Shir Simon,Zhenzhen Li,Minhao Cheng,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MM-R2提出了一种多模态智能体检索框架，通过先推理再检索的方式，在结构化知识图谱上进行意图导向的多步检索，显著提升了多模态检索增强生成的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mRAG系统直接在扁平化证据空间上检索，面临检索目标不明确（需要将问题意图与正确的视觉指代物对齐）和搜索空间弱结构（语义不同的证据在同一全局排序中竞争）两大挑战。

Method: MM-R2构建了意图导向的检索状态（包含信息需求、指代物和检索约束），并在结构化KnowledgeMap上让智能体选择相关检索单元并执行多步检索；同时构建了MM-R2-Traj轨迹数据集，采用SFT+GRPO两阶段后训练策略。

Result: 在Infoseek和Encyclopedic VQA数据集上，MM-R2在答案准确率上大幅超越强基线方法，并生成了更可解释和可验证的检索轨迹。

Conclusion: 先推理再检索、结构化搜索空间以及多步检索轨迹训练可以有效提升mRAG系统的检索质量和可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) aims to answer image-text queries with external knowledge, but most existing systems still retrieve directly from raw multimodal input over a flat evidence space. This design often struggles with two key challenges: the retrieval target is under-specified because the question intent must be grounded to the correct visual referent, and the search space is weakly structured, forcing semantically distinct evidence to compete in a single global ranking step. We propose MM-R2, a multimodal agentic retrieval framework that reasons before retrieval by explicitly modeling both what to retrieve and where to search. MM-R2 first constructs an intent-grounded retrieval state from the image-question pair, capturing the information need, grounded referent, and retrieval constraints. It then performs retrieval over a structured KnowledgeMap, where the agent selects relevant retrieval units before issuing grounded queries within them. To enable this capability, we build MM-R2-Traj, a large-scale trajectory dataset of multi-step retrieval processes, and adopt a two-stage post-training strategy with supervised fine-tuning and GRPO. Experiments on Infoseek and Encyclopedic VQA datasets show that MM-R2 substantially outperforms strong baselines on answer accuracy while also yielding more interpretable and verifiable retrieval trajectories.

</details>


### [41] [Self-Supervised Consistency Enhanced Disentangled Learning for Neural Decoding Generalization in Brain-Machine Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24023)
*Jiyu Wei,Di Hong,Zhanjie Zhang,Dazhong Rong,Qinming He,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SSCDL框架，通过一致性增强神经解码器（CND）和互补解耦泛化机制（CDG），从神经漂移中学习鲁棒的表征，显著提升脑机接口跨天的解码性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑机接口在长期使用中因神经漂移导致性能持续下降，且现有方法难以学习鲁棒的神经表征，忽略了神经漂移在不同运动参数（如速度、方向、速度大小）上的差异性。

Method: 提出SSCDL框架，包含两个核心创新：(i) 设计CND骨干模型，通过教师-学生一致性约束和模拟神经信号扰动学习对神经漂移不变的表征；(ii) 引入CDG机制，利用三个专用CND将运动信号解耦为速度、方向和速度大小三个分量，从多神经偏好视角捕获不变表征。

Result: 大量实验表明，SSCDL实现了最先进的解码性能，具备高鲁棒性和跨天稳定性。

Conclusion: SSCDL在长期人机交互、精细辅助应用等场景中展现出强大的潜力。

Abstract: Brain-Machine Interfaces (BMIs) provide a direct communication pathway between the brain and external devices, enabling humans to control assistive and robotic technologies, with potential applications in rehabilitation, human motor augmentation, and human-centered robotics. However, due to neural drift, the performance of BMIs decreases over time, posing challenges for long-term viability, particularly for invasive BMIs (iBMIs). Existing solutions suffer from two main drawbacks: (i) difficulty in learning robust neural representations, and (ii) neglecting that neural drift varies across motor parameters (e.g., velocity, direction, and speed). To overcome these limitations, we propose Self-Supervised Consistency enhanced Disentangled Learning (SSCDL), a neural decoding generalization framework built on two key innovations. We first design a backbone model named Consistency enhanced Neural Decoder (CND), using a novel teacher-student consistency constraint with simulated neural signal perturbations to learn robust representations invariant to neural drift. Then, we employ three dedicated CNDs under the Complementary-Disentangled Generalization (CDG) mechanism, which disentangles motor signals into velocity, direction, and speed with inspiration from neural preference theory. This disentangled learning enables SSCDL to capture invariant neural representations from diverse neural preference perspectives, significantly enhancing cross-day generalization. Extensive experimental results show that SSCDL delivers state-of-the-art decoding performance, exhibiting high robustness and cross-day stability. These capabilities underscore its strong potential for long-term interaction in human-centric robotic and fine-grained assistive applications.

</details>


### [42] [DocHRL: A Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning Framework for Cost-Optimised Document Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22644)
*Mohammed Yousif,Prabhjot Singh,Arjun Pankajakshan,Madhu Reddiboina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocHRL是一个基于分层强化学习的文档分类框架，能够为每个文档自适应选择最优分类策略，在RVL-CDIP基准上以2.74归一化单位成本实现0.973的宏F1分数，显著优于固定分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档分类流程对所有文档采用相同的处理模型序列，导致简单文档被过度处理、困难文档处理不足，造成计算和人力资源的低效使用。

Method: 将文档分类建模为序贯决策问题，采用两级策略层次：顶层策略选择视觉分类器、大语言模型、OCR及人工审核等宽泛选项；选项内子策略选择具体模型或工具。奖励信号为负的总期望成本（推理成本+误分类成本+人工标注成本），使用近端策略优化（PPO）进行训练。

Result: 在RVL-CDIP基准上，DocHRL在16个文档类别上达到0.973的宏F1分数，平均每文档成本降至2.74归一化单位，远低于固定独立分类器的高额成本。

Conclusion: 成本感知的强化学习能够在文档理解系统中同时提升分类性能和运行效率，证明了自适应动态策略选择的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world document classification pipelines typically apply the same sequence of models to every incoming document, regardless of its complexity or type. This leads to inefficient use of compute and human resources: simple documents are over-processed while difficult ones may not receive enough scrutiny. We introduce DocHRL, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that learns to adaptively and dynamically select the most cost-effective classification policy on a per-document basis. DocHRL formulates document classification as a sequential decision problem with a two-level policy hierarchy: a top-level policy selects among broad options (vision classifiers, LLMs, OCR, and human-in-the-loop review), while option-specific sub-policies choose the concrete model or tool to invoke. The reward signal is the negative total expected cost, which captures inference cost, cost of misclassification, and cost of human labelling. Trained with Proximal Policy Optimisation on the RVL-CDIP benchmark, DocHRL achieves a macro F1 of 0.973 across 16 document classes while reducing average per-document cost to 2.74 normalised units compared to substantially higher costs incurred by fixed standalone classifiers. Our results demonstrate that cost-aware reinforcement learning can simultaneously improve classification performance and operational efficiency in document understanding systems.

</details>


### [43] [A Cyclic Adaptation-Generalization Framework with Uncertainty-Guided Self-Paced Learning for Long-Term Brain-Machine Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24031)
*Jiyu Wei,Di Hong,Zhanjie Zhang,Dazhong Rong,Qinming He,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对侵入式脑机接口长期部署面临的神经漂移挑战，本文提出不确定性引导的自适应循环框架（UnSPC），通过循环整合领域适应与领域泛化，并结合伪标签技术，有效缓解了全局和子域分布偏移，在多个神经解码数据集上验证了其鲁棒性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侵入式脑机接口在长期部署中面临神经漂移问题，导致解码性能持续下降且需要频繁重新校准。现有方法仅单独使用领域适应或领域泛化，无法捕捉跨神经子域的细粒度分布偏移，性能受限。

Method: 提出UnSPC框架，包含两个核心组件：1）Uncertainty-guided Self-paced Pseudo-labeling (UnSPL)，通过噪声鲁棒排序策略迭代挖掘可靠伪标签样本；2）Cycling Adaptation and Generalization (CycAG)，在迭代循环中整合DA和DG，逐步缓解全局和子域漂移。

Result: 在多个神经解码数据集上的大量实验证明了UnSPC的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文首次将领域适应、领域泛化与伪标签技术以循环方式整合，为稳定的长期脑机接口控制开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Brain-Machine Interfaces (BMIs), which link the brain to external devices, hold great potential in rehabilitation, human performance augmentation, and human-centered robotics. However, invasive BMIs face a critical challenge for long-term deployment due to neural drift, which degrades decoding performance over time and necessitates frequent recalibration. Existing methods designed to mitigate neural drift typically rely on either domain adaptation (DA) or domain generalization (DG) alone and often fail to capture fine-grained distribution shifts across neural subdomains, resulting in limited performance. To overcome these limitations, we propose Uncertainty-guided Self-paced Cycling (UnSPC), a robust framework that synergizes DA and DG for target domain refining under an Uncertainty-guided Self-paced Pseudo-labeling (UnSPL) mechanism. To handle subdomain neural drift across domains, UNSPL is proposed to iteratively mine reliable pseudo-labeled samples with a noise-robust ranking strategy for further fine-tuning. Leveraging these high-quality samples, we introduce a novel Cycling Adaptation and Generalization (CycAG) strategy, which integrates DA and DG within an iterative cycle to progressively mitigate both global and subdomain drift. This cyclic process enables effective alignment to evolving target distributions while preserving robust and transferable representations, thereby mitigating performance degradation under long-term neural drifts. Extensive experiments on multiple neural decoding datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of UnSPC. To our knowledge, our proposed UnSPC is the first to cyclically integrate DA and DG with pseudo-labeling, paving the way toward stable long-term BMI controls.

</details>


### [44] [STAIF: A Stage-wise Optimization for Complex Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22649)
*Jian Hong,Chen Cheng,Quan Liu,Yuhao Chen,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STAIF框架，通过两阶段优化（先偏好优化软约束，再强化学习硬约束）提升LLM对多约束指令的遵循能力，并构建了约31K条双语复杂指令数据集STAINSTRUCT，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法（如DPO）优化整体奖励信号，在分布外或多约束场景下难以严格满足每个独立约束，尤其是客观可验证的硬约束常被忽视。

Method: STAIF采用两阶段解耦优化：Stage 1 使用多负样本偏好优化增强对主观软约束的敏感性；Stage 2 采用可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）强制满足客观硬约束。同时构建了约31K条英中双语复杂多约束指令数据集STAINSTRUCT。

Result: 在代表性基准上相比强基线取得SOTA性能，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: STAIF通过解耦软硬约束的分阶段优化有效提升了LLM在多约束指令遵循上的表现，验证了该设计思路的有效性。

Abstract: Following complex instructions with multiple explicit constraints remains a fundamental challenge for large language models (LLMs). Existing alignment methods, such as DPO, optimize holistic reward signals that often underemphasize strict satisfaction of individual constraints, particularly under out-of-distribution or multi-constraint settings. In this paper, we propose STAIF, a stage-wise optimization framework that decouples the alignment of subjective (soft) constraints from the optimization of objectively verifiable (hard) constraints. Stage 1 applies preference optimization with multiple negative samples to sharpen sensitivity to soft constraints, while Stage 2 applies Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) to enforce strict compliance with hard constraints. To support this method, we construct STAINSTRUCT, a high-quality bilingual (English, Chinese) dataset of approximately 31,000 complex multi-constraint instructions. Extensive analyses validate the design of STAIF and show state-of-the-art performance on representative benchmarks against strong baselines, as well as genuine generalization.

</details>


### [45] [ARdena: Scenario-driven control of real-time LLM agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22651)
*Luka Borozan,Domagoj Matijević*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层场景驱动的LLM控制框架，通过结构化提示在运行时控制对话代理行为，无需模型微调或对齐。该框架在实时多模态具身代理ARDena上实现，实验表明仅通过场景定义即可产生显著不同的交互行为，同时保持稳定的实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理控制方法依赖模型微调或对齐，难以适应实时交互环境中动态变化的交互需求，缺乏灵活的运行时行为控制手段。

Method: 提出分层场景驱动的LLM控制框架，结合持久化上下文与场景特定约束，通过结构化提示在运行时修改代理行为，并在ARDena（实时多模态具身代理，集成语音交互、视觉感知、工具使用和基于头像的响应生成）上实现。

Result: 仅通过场景定义即可产生显著不同的交互行为，同时保持稳定的实时操作，在控制有效性、响应延迟和运行稳定性方面均有良好表现。

Conclusion: 场景驱动提示是控制LLM代理的有效方法，能够在无需修改底层模型的前提下实现运行时的灵活行为调控。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled increasingly capable conversational agents, but reliably controlling their behavior in real-time interactive environments remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches often rely on model fine-tuning or alignment procedures that are difficult to adapt to changing interaction requirements. This paper introduces layered scenario-driven LLM control, a framework that enables runtime behavior control through structured prompting. By combining persistent context with scenario-specific constraints, the approach allows agent behavior to be modified during interaction without changing the underlying model. The framework is implemented in ARDena, a real-time multimodal embodied agent that integrates speech interaction, visual perception, tool use, and avatar-based response generation. The proposed approach is evaluated with respect to control effectiveness, response latency, and operational stability. The results demonstrate that scenario definitions alone can produce substantially different interaction behaviors while maintaining stable real-time operation, highlighting the effectiveness of scenario-driven prompting for controlling LLM agents.

</details>


### [46] [How LLM Task-Adaptation Reshapes Alignment: A Multi-dimensional Study of Behavioral and Representational Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22676)
*James Elcock,William F. Shen,Xinchi Qiu,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种后训练方法（SFT、KL-SFT、RLVR）对大型语言模型在15个对齐维度上的影响，发现RLVR在提升任务性能的同时对对齐的破坏最小，而SFT会导致显著的对齐漂移，KL正则化可部分缓解此问题。结果表明任务适配本质上也是一种对齐干预，应将对齐的多维度评估纳入标准后训练流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注后训练对安全行为的影响，但对更广泛对齐领域（如事实性、立场稳定性、社会危害、可控性、可指导性等）的系统性影响尚不明确。

Method: 对SFT、KL正则化SFT和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）三种代表性后训练方法，在涵盖安全、事实性、立场稳定性、社会危害、可控性和可指导性六大领域的15个对齐方面进行系统评估，并辅以表示层面的分析。

Result: RLVR在提升任务性能的同时仅引起较小的、非零的度量特定偏移；SFT导致各领域显著更大的对齐漂移；KL正则化可缓解漂移，但仍不如RLVR能保持对齐；表示层面的偏移与行为漂移一致。

Conclusion: 任务适配本质上是一种对齐干预，应将对齐的多维度评估作为后训练流程的标准组成部分。

Abstract: Post-training is a key mechanism for adapting large language models to downstream tasks. While prior work suggests that task adaptation can alter a model's pre-existing alignment, especially its safety behavior, its broader effects across alignment domains remain poorly understood. We address this gap through a systematic evaluation of representative task-adaptation methods, including supervised fine-tuning (SFT), KL-regularized SFT, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) across 15 alignment aspects spanning six key domains: safety, factuality, stance stability, social harm, controllability, and instructability. Our results reveal that post-training does not reshape alignment uniformly. RLVR improves task performance while inducing comparatively small, but non-zero, metric-specific shifts, while SFT leads to substantially larger alignment drift across domains. KL regularization mitigates this effect: stronger reference-model anchoring reduces alignment drift from the baseline, although KL-SFT still falls short of RLVR in preserving alignment. Representation-level analysis further supports this pattern, with shifts in alignment-relevant representations tracking behavioral drift. Together, these results show that task adaptation is not merely a capability-improving step, but an alignment intervention in its own right, motivating multi-dimensional alignment evaluation as a standard component of post-training pipelines.

</details>


### [47] [LazyMem: Retrieve Broadly, Construct Selectively for Efficient Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22690)
*Jing Yu,Yibo Zhao,Jiaming Zhang,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LazyMem 提出了一种查询时延迟构建记忆的方法，使用 4B 模型在并行窗口中选择性压缩检索结果，兼顾噪声消除与信息保留，在 LongMemEval 上以仅 213 个记忆 token 达到 0.85 的 LLM-judge 准确率，比纯检索减少了 68.7 倍 token 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 长期记忆方法面临两难：检索虽覆盖广但引入噪声，压缩虽减少噪声但可能永久丢失未来查询所需信息。LazyMem 旨在通过将记忆构建延迟到查询时刻，避免提前压缩导致的不可逆信息损失。

Method: 采用 4B 小模型在查询时对检索候选池进行重叠并行窗口处理，选择性保留并压缩与查询相关的内容。训练采用监督微调 + 基于组的强化学习，使用格式门控复合奖励函数，结合基于规则的 action signal（衡量选择准确性）与 LLM 评判的 quality signal（衡量源忠实度和查询效用）。

Result: 在 LongMemEval 上，LazyMem-4B 达到 0.85 LLM-judge 准确率，仅用 213 个记忆 token（比检索减少 68.7 倍）；32B 变体达到 0.93，在聚合型问题上超越 oracle 上下文参考；无需目标域训练即可泛化到 LoCoMo（0.68）；平均延迟低于先前查询时基线方法。

Conclusion: 查询时延迟构建记忆策略有效解决了检索-压缩两难问题，在显著降低 token 开销的同时保持或超越检索基线性能，并展现出良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets LLM agents reuse past interactions, but raw dialogue histories are verbose and information-sparse. Retrieving broadly improves evidence coverage yet overwhelms downstream reasoning with noise; compressing at write time reduces noise but irreversibly discards details the future query may need. We introduce LazyMem, which sidesteps this dilemma by deferring all memory construction to query time. A lightweight 4B model processes the retrieved candidate pool in overlapping parallel windows, selectively retaining and compressing only query-relevant content. The model is trained through supervised fine-tuning followed by group-based reinforcement learning with a format-gated composite reward that combines a rule-based action signal measuring selection accuracy with an LLM-judged quality signal measuring source faithfulness and query utility. On the LongMemEval benchmark, LazyMem-4B achieves an LLM-judge accuracy of 0.85 with only 213 memory tokens, 68.7$\times$ fewer than retrieval-only, and generalizes to LoCoMo (0.68) without target-domain training, while reducing mean latency over the prior query-time baseline. The 32B variant reaches 0.93, surpassing oracle-context references on aggregation-heavy question types. The code associated with this work is publicly available at https://github.com/allacnobug/LazyMem.

</details>


### [48] [HiLLTS: Zero-Shot Hierarchical LLM-Guided Traffic Signal Control for Sustainable Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22691)
*Yue Ding,Tendai Mukande,Mingming Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiLLTS是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的三层分层交通信号控制框架，能显著降低城市交通拥堵中的平均等待时间和CO2排放，相比最强非LLM基线在高低拥堵场景下分别减少等待时间14.71%-36.73%和排放7.87%-8.57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定时间调度、感应控制及强化学习（RL）方法存在适应性有限或需要大量重训练、精心设计奖励函数和大量仿真数据的问题，难以跨网络或需求模式迁移，亟需更高效的交通信号控制方案。

Method: 提出HiLLTS框架，采用三层分层架构：中央协调代理（中央层）、区域层和多个集群级交叉口代理（集群层），以大语言模型引导各层协调决策。

Result: 在低拥堵场景下，相比最强非LLM基线，平均等待时间降低36.73%，CO2排放降低7.87%；高拥堵场景下分别降低14.71%和8.57%。相比最弱基线，低拥堵时排放最高降低18.00%，等待时间最高降低62.07%；高拥堵时排放最高降低28.89%，等待时间最高降低40.36%。消融实验验证了LLM引导协调优于基于规则的控制。

Conclusion: HiLLTS框架通过LLM引导的分层协调机制，在跨场景交通信号控制中同时改善拥堵和环境影响，且无需大量重训练，为智能交通系统提供了高效、鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion significantly increases fuel consumption, greenhouse gas emissions, and commuter delays, resulting in substantial economic losses and environmental harm in modern cities. Traditional traffic signal control strategies such as fixed-time scheduling, actuated control, and reinforcement learning (RL)-based methods, offer different degrees of adaptability; however, RL-based methods can require extensive retraining, careful reward design, and substantial simulation data when transferred across networks or demand regimes. To address these challenges, we propose HiLLTS, an LLM-guided traffic signal control framework that employs a hierarchical three-layer architecture consisting of a central coordination agent, a district layer and multiple cluster-level intersection agents. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements in both congestion and environmental performance. Compared with the strongest non-LLM baseline in each scenario, HiLLTS reduces average waiting time by 36.73% under the low-congestion scenario and 14.71% under the high-congestion scenario, while reducing average CO2 emissions by 7.87% and 8.57%, respectively. Larger gains are observed against weaker baselines: under low congestion, HiLLTS achieves reductions of up to 18.00% in emissions and 62.07% in waiting time relative to Fixed-Time control; under high congestion, reductions of up to 28.89% in emissions and 40.36% in waiting time are observed relative to Max Pressure. The ablation study further validates the contribution of LLM-guided coordination over rule-based control

</details>


### [49] [Test-Time Coverage: Test-Conditioned Data Curation for Deployment-Aware Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22697)
*Nadine Chang,Maying Shen,Shizhe Diao,Jialiang Wang,Jingde Chen,Thomas Breuel,Pavlo Molchanov,Rafid Mahmood,Jose M. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TTCov（测试时覆盖）方法，在训练前利用测试集信息进行数据筛选，无需推理时更新模型权重。通过构建知识图谱（K-Atlas）描述部署分布，并据此选择训练数据，在自动驾驶任务中优于现有数据筛选方法，且能适应新领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据筛选方法仅基于训练侧标准评分，未能直接优化与部署测试分布的匹配度，导致部署性能不佳。

Method: TTCov将部署条件化数据筛选分解为覆盖度和分布两个维度：1）构建任务Atlas——基于LLM的原子命题集合描述部署相关概念，从开放任务知识种子出发，用未标注部署样本中不匹配的原子命题扩充；2）知识图谱（K-Atlas）——将匹配的部署原子命题按其频率实例化，将部署分布作为筛选目标；3）选择预算内训练集，使其部署原子命题分布逼近K-Atlas目标分布。

Result: 在自动驾驶任务中，TTCov相比基线数据筛选方法，实现了更大的部署相关覆盖度、更接近的K-Atlas匹配度，以及更强的端到端驾驶性能，并能通过城市间扩展无缝适应新领域。

Conclusion: TTCov通过测试时信息指导训练数据筛选，在不影响推理效率的前提下有效提升部署匹配度，为数据层面适应测试分布提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deployed AI systems are often trained from broad candidate data pools, necessitating data curation towards the deployment test distribution. However, standard data curation methods score training-side criteria rather than directly optimizing deployment match. We introduce TTCov (Test-Time Coverage), a data-level test-conditioned curation method that uses test-side information before training instead of updating model weights at inference. TTCov decomposes deployment-conditioned curation into coverage and distribution. To represent coverage, it builds a task Atlas, a collection of LLM-based atomic propositions (APs) describing deployment-relevant concepts, seeded from open task knowledge and expanded with unmatched APs extracted from unlabeled deployment samples. To represent distribution, it instantiates the matched deployment APs with their frequencies, yielding a Knowledge Atlas (K-Atlas) that operationalizes the deployment distribution as a curation target. TTCov then selects a budgeted training set whose deployment APs distribution approximates this target. We apply TTCov towards autonomous driving (AD), keeping adaptation off the inference path while selecting data with greater deployment-relevant coverage, closer K-Atlas matching, and stronger downstream end-to-end driving performance than data-curation baselines, including seamless adaptability to novel domains via city-to-city expansion.

</details>


### [50] [AgentOmnia: Scaling Agentic Models for Full-Scenario Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23124)
*Hao Jiang,Gangtao Xin,Yingdi Huang,Guojie Zhu,Jiangshan Zhang,Xinyuan Lin,Yunkun Xu,Chengyu Shen,Wenlong Fei,Jiawei Li,Yujie Fu,Sichen Kang,Tingyu Xie,Yedi Hu,Jingren Zhang,Hongcheng Gao,Jianshu Zeng,Chong Chen,Chang Guo,Chao Feng,Feng Wang,Fulin Lin,Jinchao Ma,Lang Mei,Li Huang,Liyan Liu,Qing He,Shuting Tao,Siyu Mo,Xiangnan Chen,Xiaohan Yu,Xiaoyang Li,Yanheng Hou,Yanyu Wu,Zhihan Yang,Wentao Zhang,Yang Gao,Zhao Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentOmnia是一个全场景智能体扩展框架，通过领域×能力×原子难度分类法、双向环境-任务合成、在线强化学习等机制，在ToC/ToB/ToE三大应用场景中显著提升LLM智能体的性能，关键指标从9.16%提升至37.11%（挑战集通过率），并在多个基准上超越更大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的进展在领域、能力、任务难度和交互设置上呈现碎片化，缺乏一个统一的全场景框架来协调数据合成、后训练、评估和改进的各个环节。

Method: 提出AgentOmnia框架，包含可扩展的领域×能力×原子难度分类法、双向环境-任务合成（结合工具依赖、程序结构和求解器流水线）、后训练（监督微调+在线智能体强化学习+回滚课程）、以及基于PRD的自我进化机制。

Result: 在OmniaBench挑战集上通过率从9.16%提升至37.11%，四个基准宏平均从22.86%提升至41.69%；超越Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507在所有四个基准上的表现；提升覆盖90个一级领域中的76个、10个能力维度和8个原子难度因素。

Conclusion: AgentOmnia实现了全场景智能体扩展，验证了统一框架在不同应用场景下的有效性，初步证明了PRD引导自我进化的可行性，为更大规模和工业场景的应用提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language model agents have advanced rapidly, yet progress remains fragmented across domains, capabilities, task difficulty, and interaction settings. We frame this as full-scenario agentic scaling and present AgentOmnia, a framework coordinating task-space definition, data synthesis, post-training, evaluation, and improvement across To-Consumer (ToC), To-Business (ToB), and To-Employee (ToE) applications. An extensible Domain x Capability x Atomic Difficulty taxonomy aligns these stages and enables fine-grained diagnosis with OmniaBench. AgentOmnia combines bidirectional environment-task synthesis with tool-dependency, program-structured, and solver-based pipelines, constructing 5,018 stateful environments with 255,375 tools and 52,361 tasks. Programs, solvers, and verifiers provide correctness signals, while supervised fine-tuning, online agentic reinforcement learning, and a rollback curriculum support post-training. Evaluation failures translate into Product Requirement Documents (PRDs) for targeted self-evolution. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking-2507, AgentOmnia raises the pass rate on the OmniaBench challenging subset from 9.16% to 37.11% and the macro-average across OmniaBench, $τ^2$-Bench, DeepPlanning, and VitaBench from 22.86% to 41.69%. Under a unified protocol,it leads the evaluated agentic post-trained baselines on OmniaBench and retains the highest four-benchmark macro-average. It also surpasses Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507 on all four benchmarks and exceeds Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on the macro-average. Gains span three application splits, ten capability dimensions, eight atomic-difficulty factors, and 76 of 90 level-1 domains, indicating broad rather than category-specific improvement. A one-round study provides initial evidence for PRD-guided self-evolution, motivating validation at larger scales and in industrial settings.

</details>


### [51] [SeekJudge: A Practical Reward Framework for Reinforcement Learning in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23263)
*Yang Wan,Zhenhao Zhang,Jierui Wang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SeekJudge框架，通过四个角色专业化智能体（Condense、Ground、Seek、Analyze）组成的Seek-Analyze循环来评判GUI轨迹是否遵循指令，并采用种子校准蒸馏管道训练一个共享的9B模型作为骨干，首次在在线强化学习中达到或超越原生规则监督的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的评估难以对齐人类意图，且在应用更新或内容漂移时失效；现有基于模型的评估器虽试图解决这些问题，但与规则评估之间仍存在性能差距。

Method: SeekJudge框架包含四个角色专业化智能体：Condense（压缩轨迹）、Ground（将指令与界面元素对齐）、Seek（搜索关键证据）、Analyze（分析并做出裁决），通过Seek-Analyze循环迭代判断；采用种子校准蒸馏管道训练一个9B模型作为四个智能体的共享骨干网络。

Result: 在保留的RL测试目标上，SeekJudge是首个在在线RL中达到或超越原生规则监督的实用的基于模型奖励函数；提供步骤级判断，运行成本远低于闭源大模型，保持小上下文从而可扩展至更长轨迹。

Conclusion: SeekJudge使基于模型的奖励成为CUA强化学习中规则监督的实用替代方案；同时贡献了奖励服务器的一般性架构改进以加速RL中的评判过程。

Abstract: Deciding whether a trajectory actually fulfills its instruction governs how we measure computer-use agents on long-horizon graphical-user-interface tasks and how we train them with reinforcement learning. This judgment has long relied on rule-based evaluation, which struggles to align with human intention and goes stale when an app updates or its online content drifts. Existing model-based judges attempt to address these problems but still leave a performance gap to the rule-based evaluation. We propose the \textbf{SeekJudge} framework, in which four role-specialized agents, a Condense, a Ground, a Seek and an Analyze agent, reach a verdict through a Seek--Analyze loop over the trajectory. A seed-calibrated distillation pipeline trains one specialized $9$B model to serve as the shared backbone for all four agents. Measured by downstream success rate on held-out RL test goals, SeekJudge is the first practical model-based reward to match or surpass native rule-based supervision in online RL. Beyond accuracy, SeekJudge provides step-level judgments, runs far cheaper than a closed-source large model, and keeps a small per-call context that scales to much longer trajectories. We further contribute a general architectural improvement to the reward server that speeds up judging in RL. Together these make model-based reward a practical drop-in for rule-based supervision in CUA reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [52] [ObsDriveBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Understanding under Adverse Weather with Observability Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23537)
*Qiao Yan,Yihan Wang,Zhenghao Xing,Jiaqi Xu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ObsDriveBench，一个面向恶劣天气下自动驾驶的真实多模态基准，包含可观测性感知、空间可靠性和风险感知决策三个能力维度，并引入ObsDrive模型通过正常天气监督微调和恶劣天气强化学习提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言基准主要在标准条件、合成退化或单模态下评估，无法真实反映多模态模型在恶劣天气下的表现，核心难点在于环境可观测性退化导致多模态观测不可靠且跨模态不一致。

Method: 通过可观测性元标注、场景描述和能力导向的多选任务构建基准，包含14K训练和13K测试问题，覆盖相机、LiDAR和雷达同步输入；ObsDrive模型采用正常天气监督微调+恶劣天气强化学习的训练策略。

Result: 现有视觉-语言模型在恶劣天气下性能持续下降，ObsDrive模型在所有三个能力维度上均提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 恶劣天气下多模态观测退化是影响自动驾驶场景理解与决策的关键瓶颈，提出的基准和模型为提升恶劣天气鲁棒性提供了有效工具和方法。

Abstract: Autonomous driving under adverse weather remains a critical challenge, yet existing vision-language benchmarks mainly evaluate under standard conditions, synthetic corruptions, or single modality. As a result, it remains unclear how vision-language models behave under real-world adverse weather with multi-modal inputs. We argue that a key difficulty lies in degraded environmental observability: under fog, rain, snow, and low illumination, multi-modal observations become unreliable and cross-modally inconsistent, posing challenges to scene understanding, and subsequent decision-making. To study this, we introduce \textbf{ObsDriveBench}, a real-world multi-modal benchmark for adverse-weather autonomous driving. Our benchmark is designed with three capability dimensions: \textbf{observability awareness}, \textbf{spatial reliability}, and \textbf{risk-aware decision-making}, enabling fine-grained diagnosis of model behavior under degraded observations. We construct the benchmark through observability meta-annotation, scene description, and capability oriented multiple-choice tasks over synchronized camera, LiDAR, and radar inputs, forming a benchmark with over 14k training and 13k test questions. Experiments reveal consistent performance degradation of existing vision-language models. We further introduce \textbf{ObsDrive} model with normal-weather supervised fine-tuning and adverse-weather reinforcement learning, improving robustness across all three capabilities. The dataset and evaluation code will be released at \href{https://github.com/russellyq/ObsDriveBench}{\texttt{ObsDriveBench}}.

</details>


### [53] [Hybrid Advantage Estimation with Unified Critic for VLM Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23605)
*Wenxuan Zhang,Yuhui Wang,Donggang Jia,Xiaoqian Shen,Jian Ding,Ivan Viola,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HyGAE，一个在视觉语言多轮决策中联合优化token级和turn级目标的Actor-Critic框架，通过混合优势函数和统一评论家模型，在五个环境中取得91%平均成功率，比现有方法提升10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多轮决策VLMs的优化方法要么进行token级优化（拼接整个轨迹做逐token优化），要么进行turn级优化（回合内均匀分配信用），缺乏对两个粒度目标的统一理论框架和联合优化方法。

Method: 1. 建立token级和turn级优化的理论公式；2. 推导出同时服务于两个目标的混合优势函数（Hybrid Advantage）；3. 证明通过合适的折扣因子和学习目标，一个统一的评论家模型可以同时估计turn级和token级价值；4. 提出HyGAE Actor-Critic框架，联合优化两个粒度的目标。

Result: 在五个多轮决策环境中平均成功率达91%，相比其他方法提升10%。消融实验验证了混合优势函数和分析形式的返回值的必要性。

Conclusion: HyGAE通过统一的评论家模型和混合优势函数，有效联合优化了token级和turn级目标，显著提升了VLM在多轮交互环境中的决策能力。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) now act as agents in interactive environments, where success requires coherent reasoning and decision-making across turns. Although end-to-end training in agentic environments can improve such multi-turn decision-making abilities, current methods mainly rely on either token-wise optimization over concatenated token trajectories or turn-wise optimization with uniform within-turn credit. In this work, we establish theoretical formulations for the two levels of optimization and derive a hybrid advantage that serves both objectives. Furthermore, with an appropriate choice of discount factor and learning target, we prove that a unified critic model can estimate values for both turn-wise and token-wise. As such, we propose HyGAE, an actor-critic framework that jointly optimizes token- and turn-level objectives with the hybrid advantage and unified critic. We conduct extensive evaluations of HyGAE across five multi-turn decision-making environments, where it achieves an average success rate of 91% and a significant improvement of 10% over other methods. Furthermore, we provide an in-depth analysis showing that the exact analytic form of the hybrid advantage and return is crucial for optimization. Project Page: https://wx-zhang.github.io/hygae-web/.

</details>


### [54] [Offline-Online Curriculum RL for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23700)
*Wendi Deng,Hang Du,Guoshun Nan,Haokun Tian,Jiaqi Yu,Xinlei Cao,Jaile Li,Jingfeng Chen,Ling Deng,Ting Li,Hao Yang,Jun Liu,Xudong Jiang,Sicong Leng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出O²-CritiCuRL框架，通过课程强化学习与离线-在线迭代范式，让多模态大模型聚焦关键推理步骤、过滤冗余步骤，在提升可解释性和可靠性的同时实现SOTA性能与高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在推理任务中常出现中间步骤有误但最终答案正确的情况，暴露出模型依赖虚假捷径而非忠实推理，降低了可解释性与可靠性。现有步级监督方法难以区分关键步骤与冗余步骤。

Method: 提出O²-CritiCuRL框架：(1) 离线阶段：对带步骤标注的轨迹进行多次 rollout 分析，估计步级重要性，蒸馏关键推理步骤并过滤冗余步骤；(2) 在线阶段：采用渐进式步级强化学习策略，通过截断链引导模型推断缺失步骤并优化推理，强化对关键步骤的关注。

Result: 在多模态推理基准上取得SOTA性能，并在训练和推理效率上均表现优越。

Conclusion: O²-CritiCuRL通过引入关键步骤感知的课程强化学习，有效解决了多模态大模型推理中的虚假捷径问题，在提升推理可解释性和可靠性的同时兼顾了效率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models exhibit capabilities on reasoning tasks, yet often produce flawed intermediate steps while yielding correct final answers. This behavior undermines interpretability and reliability, suggesting reliance on spurious shortcuts rather than faithful reasoning. Although efforts have explored step-level supervision, distinguishing decisive steps from redundant ones remains challenging. We propose $O^2$-CritiCuRL, a novel curriculum reinforcement learning framework that introduces critical-step awareness through an iterative offline-online paradigm. In the offline stage, $O^2$-CritiCuRL conducts multi-rollout analysis over step-annotated trajectories to estimate step-level importance, allowing the framework to distill critical reasoning steps and filter out redundant ones. In the online stage, we employ a progressive step-level reinforcement learning strategy, where truncated chains guide the model to infer missing steps and refine its reasoning, thereby sharpening its focus on critical steps and overcoming the limitations of static supervision. Extensive experiments on multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance while delivering superior training and inference efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/kk0013/CritiCuRL.

</details>


### [55] [Training Language Models to Cooperate with Inference-Time Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23771)
*Moumita Choudhury,Vanshaj Khattar,Jing Liu,Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Ankush Chakrabarty,Shlomo Zilberstein,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CALM提出了一种控制器感知的后训练框架，通过在训练过程中显式引入多种推理控制器（如CoT、自一致性、辩论等），解决传统单控制器训练与多控制器部署环境不匹配的问题，从而提升模型对未知控制器组合的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法通常针对单一固定交互模式进行优化，但实际部署中依赖多种控制器（如思维链、自一致性、辩论、规划、验证流水线等），导致训练与部署不匹配，限制了向新工作流的迁移。

Method: 提出控制器感知的后训练框架CALM，将控制器显式纳入训练循环；将控制器感知后训练建模为多任务强化学习问题，其中控制器是可复用的局部推理模块的组合；引入基于turn-level GRPO目标的模块级分解，实现混合控制器训练。

Result: 在未见过的控制器组合和更广泛的控制器迁移场景下进行评估，结果表明控制器感知后训练相比单控制器优化显著提升了跨推理工作流的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CALM通过让模型在训练阶段接触多样化的控制器，有效弥合了训练与部署之间的鸿沟，提升了语言模型对不同推理工作流的适应性和迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) performance increasingly depends not only on the base model, but also on the inference-time controller used to organize reasoning. Existing post-training methods, however, typically optimize for a single fixed interaction pattern, despite real deployments relying on diverse controllers such as Chain-of-Thought, self-consistency, debate, planning, and verification pipelines. This creates a training--deployment mismatch and limits transfer to new workflows. We introduce CALM (Controller-Aware Language Models), a post-training framework that explicitly places controllers in the training loop. We formulate controller-aware post-training as multi-task reinforcement learning over controller-induced interaction protocols, where controllers are compositions of reusable local reasoning modules. This structure also induces a module-level decomposition of mixed-controller training under a turn-level GRPO objective, enabling a systematic study of controller and module-aware training strategies. We evaluate CALM on held-out controller compositions and broader controller shifts, showing that controller-aware post-training improves generalization across inference-time workflows beyond single-controller optimization.

</details>


### [56] [From RLVR to RLSVR: Task Transformation Induces Self-Verifiable Rewards for Open-Ended LLM Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23802)
*Qinsi Wang,Jing Shi,Huazheng Wang,Kun Wan,Yiran Wu,Bo Liu,Qingyun Wu,Hai Helen Li,Yiran Chen,Handong Zhao,Wentian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLSVR框架，通过将开放任务转化为可验证的代理环境（如SpyRL多智能体自博弈），使得基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）能够扩展到数学和编程之外的开放任务，在文本摘要、创意写作和数学推理上均优于现有自改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅适用于数学和编程等可确定性验证的领域，而开放任务依赖人类偏好、奖励模型或LLM评判器，存在评估偏差、评判能力瓶颈和额外推理成本等问题。

Method: 提出RLSVR（Reinforcement Learning with Self-Verifiable Rewards），基于自监督学习思想，通过任务转换（task transformation）将开放任务转化为可验证的代理环境。具体实例化为SpyRL，一个受"谁是卧底"启发的多智能体自博弈环境：智能体接收不对称信息，完成相同目标任务后投票识别指定卧底，投票结果提供完全可验证的奖励信号。

Result: 在文本摘要、创意写作和数学推理任务上，SpyRL均优于现有的非可验证任务自改进方法，并在可验证的推理任务上也取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 任务转换策略能够将可扩展的RLVR自改进范式从固有可验证领域推广到更广泛的开放任务，扩展了强化学习在LLM优化中的应用边界。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has driven recent progress in reasoning-oriented large language models (LLMs) by enabling large-scale optimization. However, its applicability remains largely limited to domains such as mathematics and coding, where correctness can be deterministically verified. Open-ended tasks instead often rely on human preferences, reward models, or LLM-based judges, introducing evaluation bias, judge capability bottlenecks, and additional inference costs.Drawing on the principle of self-supervised learning, which constructs pretext tasks to derive supervision from the data itself, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Self-Verifiable Rewards (RLSVR), a task-transformation-based training paradigm for extending RLVR to open-ended tasks. RLSVR transforms open-ended tasks into verifiable proxy environments whose internal rules and interaction outcomes automatically generate reward signals. We instantiate RLSVR with SpyRL, a multi-agent self-play environment inspired by Who Is the Spy?. Agents receive asymmetric information, complete the same target task, and vote to identify a designated spy. Because the spy identity is predetermined, voting outcomes provide fully verifiable rewards, while successful identification remains closely related to output quality. Experiments on text summarization, creative writing, and mathematical reasoning show that SpyRL outperforms existing self-improvement methods on non-verifiable tasks and yields consistent gains on verifiable reasoning tasks. These results demonstrate that task transformation can extend scalable RLVR-based self-improvement beyond inherently verifiable domains. Models and code have been released at https://github.com/wangqinsi1/SpyRL.

</details>


### [57] [Understanding Human-like Solutions in Combinatorial Optimization via Learning and Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23854)
*Haijiang Yan,Jian-Qiao Zhu,Liqiang Huang,Ming Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人类在欧几里得旅行商问题（TSP）中能够快速生成接近最优的解。本研究通过大规模行为和计算实验，收集人类解并与基于Pointer Networks的神经策略进行比较。结果表明，人类解并非直接模仿最优解，而是通过最优监督预训练、强化学习微调及Best-of-N采样解码得到的模型最能解释人类行为，揭示了结构化监督学习、强化学习和测试时搜索的组合可能催生类人解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类如何在计算资源有限的条件下解决计算上困难的组合优化问题（如欧几里得TSP），并理解什么样的解是“类人”的，以及这类解是如何被学习到的。

Method: 大规模采样TSP实例空间，收集人类解；使用基于Pointer Networks的递归神经网络（带注意力机制的指向机制）定义有效路径的概率分布；分别使用强化学习（RL）、最优解监督学习、人类解监督学习以及最优监督预训练后进行RL微调等多种目标训练网络。

Result: 人类解与最优解不完全相同，但位于一个近最优的几何盆地中，共享许多结构属性，同时存在系统性的类人偏差。最佳解释人类解的模型是最优监督预训练后经RL微调、再通过Best-of-N采样解码的模型，而非直接模仿最优解的模型。

Conclusion: 类人解决方案可能源于结构化监督学习、强化学习和测试时搜索的组合，这呼应了许多现代人工智能系统背后的计算原理。

Abstract: Humans often find good solutions to combinatorial optimization problems that are computationally hard even for advanced computer algorithms. In the Euclidean traveling salesman problems (TSP), people rapidly produce tours that are near-optimal, despite severe limits on time and computation. What makes a tour human-like, and how might such solutions be learned? Here we address these questions through a large-scale behavioral and computational investigation of human performance in Euclidean TSP. We sampled a broad space of TSP instances, collected human solutions, and compared them with neural policies based on Pointer Networks, which are recurrent neural networks with an attention-based pointing mechanism that define probability distributions over valid tours. We trained these networks under multiple objectives, including reinforcement learning (RL), supervised learning from optimal tours, supervised learning from human tours, and RL fine-tuning after optimal-supervised pretraining. Human tours were not identical to optimal tours, but occupied a near-optimal geometric basin: they shared many structural properties with optimal solutions while preserving systematic human-specific deviations. The best account of human tours was not direct imitation of optimal tours, but a model pretrained on optimal tours, fine-tuned by RL, and decoded through $\text{Best-of-}N$ sampling. These findings suggest that human-like solutions may emerge from a combination of structured supervised learning, RL, and test-time search, echoing computational principles underlying many modern artificial intelligence systems.

</details>


### [58] [From Cognitive Architectures to Language Agents: A Mechanism-Level Review of Lineage, Convergence, and Migration Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23942)
*Haodi Fan,Zucong Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对十个历史认知架构、八个语言代理运行时家族和四十二个现代系统进行了系统梳理，通过状态、控制、转换、持久性、故障、学习和资源治理七个维度重构每个机制，并编码证据关系（E1-E4）和迁移深度（D0-D4）。研究发现现代代理已实现自适应记忆、故障恢复、动态团队选择等大量机制，但多为独立趋同而非继承。识别出五个未解决的机制耦合束，并提出了一个可证伪的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常以"记忆"、"规划"、"反思"、"工具使用"等模糊的特征标签来描述代理系统，掩盖了决定代理实际运行方式的控制语义。本文旨在通过统一的机制分析框架，揭示不同代理系统在底层控制机制上的异同，填补系统化的比较研究空白。

Method: 采用两步式分析方法：首先通过状态（state）、控制（control）、转换（transition）、持久性（persistence）、故障（failure）、学习（learning）和资源治理（resource governance）七个维度重构每个机制；然后分别编码证据关系（E1-E4，衡量机制实现的证据强度）和迁移深度（D0-D4，衡量机制从历史架构到现代系统的传承程度）。

Result: 现代代理在自适应记忆、故障恢复、动态团队选择、工作流搜索、技能归纳、资源调度和不确定性条件行动等方面取得了实质性进展，但多数是通过独立趋同而非文献可追溯的继承实现的。通过最近基线筛选发现GraSP已具备校准的多技能选择、类型化编译、验证、有界修复和重新规划或ReAct回退等能力。识别出五个未解决的机制耦合束。

Conclusion: 贡献了一个独特的机制目录、一个可审计的证据深度框架，以及一个可证伪的研究议程，用于测试五个识别出的机制耦合束作为可组合的运行时不变性。这为未来语言代理系统的系统化设计和比较提供了基础。

Abstract: Memory, planning, reflection, and tool use are often compared as feature labels, obscuring the control semantics that determine how an agent actually runs. This review connects ten historical cognitive architectures, eight language-agent runtime families, and forty-two mechanism-focused modern systems. We reconstruct each mechanism through state, control, transition, persistence, failure, learning, and resource governance, then code evidence relation (E1-E4) separately from migration depth (D0-D4). The resulting landscape is uneven. Modern agents have operationalized substantial parts of adaptive memory, failure recovery, dynamic team selection, workflow search, skill induction, resource scheduling, and uncertainty-conditioned action, although often through independent convergence rather than documented inheritance. The strongest remaining opportunities lie in couplings among mechanisms. Closest-baseline screening closes one proposed gap: GraSP already combines calibrated multi-skill selection, typed compilation, verification, bounded repair, and replanning or ReAct fallback. Five residual bundles remain: activation with latency and action utility; typed impasse with isolated substates and resolution compilation; bounded content competition with broadcast and admission learning; persistent intention with reconsideration and live method authority; and uncertainty with resource allocation, interruption, and stopping. We contribute a distinctive-mechanism catalog, an auditable evidence-depth framework, and a falsifiable agenda for testing these bundles as composable runtime invariants.

</details>


### [59] [EviBack: Search-Agent Reinforcement Learning via Evidence-Constrained Teacher Backoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23955)
*Xiao Ma,Zhiquan Hu,Yi Wei,Chenchen Zhao,Yijun Chen,Jicheng Zhao,Yuming Li Chuang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EviBack方法，通过证据约束的教师回退机制为强化学习中的零奖励组提供辅助监督，并利用自动化APE流程生成双阶段教师模型，在多个开放域QA基准上提升F1分数和有效回答率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练Agentic RAG系统时，全零奖励的rollout组缺乏比较信号，可能隐藏有价值搜索行为，需要额外的监督信号来利用这些组。

Method: 1) EviBack: 证据约束的Teacher回退机制，分离证据评估与答案精炼，为全零奖励组提供辅助监督；2) 全自动GPT-5.5辅助APE流程，从单提示双任务Teacher出发，自动分区标注rollout数据，进行消融、任务分解、评估和选择，生成门控双阶段Teacher。

Result: 在7个开放域QA基准和3种Qwen3规模上，EviBack相比Search-R1提升F1，提升单跳和多跳macro F1；相比人工设计，下游F1和有效回答率提升，搜索次数、重复查询和强制终止减少。

Conclusion: EviBack通过证据约束回退和自动化教师演化，有效强化了Agentic RAG系统的多轮搜索学习能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables Agentic RAG systems to learn multi-turn search from verifiable outcome rewards, but all- zero rollout groups provide no comparative signal and may hide useful search behavior. We present EviBack, an evidence- constrained Teacher backoff that supplies auxiliary super- vision to such groups while preserving verifiable Actor re- wards. It separates evidence assessment from answer refine- ment, preventing reference answers from overriding evidence- insufficiency judgments. A fully automated, end-to-end GPT- 5.5-assisted APE pipeline starts from a manually authored single-prompt dual-task Teacher, automatically partitions and labels rollout data, and performs ablation, task decomposition, evaluation, and selection to produce a gated two-stage Teacher. Compared with the manual design, the resulting Teacher im- proves downstream F1 and valid-answer rate while reduc- ing search, duplicate queries, and forced termination. Across seven open-domain QA benchmarks and three Qwen3 scales, EviBack improves F1 over Search-R1 and raises both single- and multi-hop macro F1. We guarantee that the code will be made publicly available at a later stage.

</details>


### [60] [MemChain: Learning Interpretable Memory Traces for Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24097)
*Yiwen Ma,Songjun Tu,Qichao Zhang,Dong Li,Linjing Li,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemChain提出了一种可训练的后检索记忆策略，将检索到的候选记忆转化为紧凑且可溯源的主动记忆（active memory），通过生成证据计划、构建有序证据轨迹、执行显式记忆操作来精简上下文，并采用两阶段学习框架（监督轨迹学习+TMPO强化学习）优化策略，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上取得SOTA性能，同时大幅减少传递给答案模型的记忆上下文量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索即证据范式假设检索到的记忆已适合推理，但实际存在冗余、冲突、弱相关性等问题，且在长期记忆任务中带来大量上下文开销，答案模型需自行处理这些噪声。

Method: 1) 问题条件证据计划生成；2) 有序可溯源的证据轨迹构建（按语义角色和依赖关系组织）；3) 显式记忆操作执行，产出精简证据上下文。训练采用两阶段：监督轨迹学习+Trace-Guided Memory Policy Optimization (TMPO) 强化学习，联合优化下游答案质量、轨迹可溯源、证据支持、结构有效性及答案稳定性。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上，MemChain在闭源和开源冻结答案模型上均取得SOTA性能，同时显著减少了传递给答案模型的记忆上下文量。

Conclusion: 后检索记忆策略能够有效解决检索记忆的噪声问题，通过结构化压缩和强化学习优化，既提升答案质量又降低上下文开销，为长期记忆LLM智能体提供了更高效的记忆使用范式。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents typically answer queries by retrieving relevant memories and feeding them directly to an answer model. This retrieval-as-evidence paradigm assumes retrieved memories are already suitable for reasoning, leaving the answer model to resolve redundancy, conflicts, and weak relevance while incurring substantial context overhead in long-term memory tasks. We propose MemChain, a trainable post-retrieval memory policy that transforms retrieved candidates into answer-facing active memory, represented as a compact and grounded evidence context. Given a user query and retrieved candidates, MemChain first generates a question-conditioned evidence plan, then constructs an ordered grounded evidence trace that organizes retrieved memories according to their semantic roles and dependencies, and finally executes explicit memory actions to produce a concise evidence context for answer generation. To train the mediator, we introduce a two-stage learning framework. Supervised trace learning first teaches the policy to generate structurally valid plans, traces, actions, and evidence contexts. We then propose Trace-Guided Memory Policy Optimization (TMPO), a reinforcement learning objective that optimizes the memory policy using downstream answer quality while jointly encouraging trace grounding, evidence support, structural validity, and answer stability across multiple rollouts. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S demonstrate that MemChain consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance across both closed-source and open-weight frozen answer models while substantially reducing the memory context passed to the answer model.

</details>


### [61] [Scaling GUI Agents with Visual State Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24112)
*Xiangyan Liu,Kaixin Li,Haonan Wang,Biao Wu,Meng Fang,Longxu Dou,Chao Du,Michael Qizhe Shieh,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出状态转换预训练（STP）作为GUI智能体的新扩展轴，通过联合优化逆动力学和正动力学，在多模态模型上持续预训练视觉状态转换，从而获得更好的动作基础视觉表征和GUI动态内部世界模型，在下游任务中持续优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体在预训练阶段缺乏对GUI动态和状态转换的显式建模，导致模型对动作与状态变化之间的因果关系理解不足，限制了其在复杂GUI场景中的泛化能力和决策质量。

Method: STP方法包含两个核心目标：逆动力学（从状态变化预测动作）和正动力学（从当前状态和动作预测下一状态），联合优化使多模态模型学习到动作锚定的视觉表征和GUI动态世界模型，然后在任务指令轨迹上进行微调。

Result: 在AgentNetBench、AndroidControl和GUIOdyssey三个基准上，STP训练的模型在桌面和移动端GUI场景中均一致优于直接轨迹微调的基线；联合动力学优化优于单一目标训练；下游性能随转换数据量稳定提升。

Conclusion: STP为GUI智能体提供了一种有效的新预训练范式，通过状态转换学习获得可扩展的性能提升，验证了动力学联合预训练在GUI智能体中的重要价值。

Abstract: We introduce State Transition Pretraining (STP) as a new scaling axis for GUI agents. During the STP stage, we continually pretrain a unified multimodal model on visual state transitions by jointly optimizing inverse dynamics (predicting actions from state changes) and forward dynamics (predicting next states from current states and actions). This optimization equips the model with better action-grounded visual representations and an internal world model of GUI dynamics. When subsequently fine-tuned on trajectories with task instructions, our STP-trained models consistently outperform baselines trained solely via direct trajectory fine-tuning across agent benchmarks in both desktop and mobile GUI scenarios (AgentNetBench, AndroidControl, and GUIOdyssey). Further empirical studies show that joint dynamics optimization yields stable improvements over single-objective training, and downstream performance scales steadily with the volume of transition data.

</details>


### [62] [A Motion-Aware Vector Quantization Framework with Centroid Reuse for Efficient VLA Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24148)
*Zhuoran Song,Haozhe Jiang,Chunyu Qi,Minnan Pei,Gang Li,Xiaoyao Liang,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VQVLA是一个算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过运动感知向量量化（MotionVQ）和合并质心向量化GEMM范式，利用权重相似性和执行动态性加速VLA模型推理，在A100 GPU、Dadu-Corki等基线方法上实现1.9x-6.5x加速，且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在GPU上推理延迟高，难以满足实时部署需求；现有加速器（如Dadu-Corki）将VLA模型视为全精度负载，未充分挖掘存储和计算中的冗余。

Method: 1) MotionVQ：运动感知向量量化方案，根据机器人执行状态动态调整量化精度；2) 合并质心向量化GEMM范式：基于码本索引表示，通过质心的空间聚合和时间复用消除冗余乘法；3) 支持动态精度选择和质心复用计算的专用加速器设计。

Result: 在A100 GPU、Dadu-Corki、LUT-DLA、CodeGEMM、ShiftAddLLM上分别实现6.5x、2.8x、1.9x、3.3x和4.3x的加速，精度下降可忽略。

Conclusion: 算法-硬件协同设计能有效挖掘VLA模型中的冗余（权重相似性和执行动态性），显著提升推理速度，适用于机器人实时控制等场景。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong potential for embodied AI, yet their high inference latency on GPUs limits real-time deployment. Existing accelerators, such as Dadu-Corki, improve efficiency but treat VLA models as full-precision workloads, leaving substantial redundancy in both memory and computation underexploited.
  In this paper, we propose VQVLA, an algorithm-hardware co-design framework that accelerates VLA inference by exploiting weight similarity and execution dynamics. We first introduce MotionVQ, a motion-aware vector quantization scheme that dynamically adjusts quantization precision based on the robot's execution state, reducing memory access while preserving task success rate. We then propose a merged-centroid vectorized GEMM paradigm that operates on the codebook-index representation, eliminating redundant multiplications through spatial aggregation and temporal reuse of centroids. To realize these optimizations, we design an accelerator that efficiently supports dynamic precision selection and centroid-reuse computation. Experimental results show that VQVLA achieves 6.5x, 2.8x, 1.9x, 3.3x, and 4.3x speedup over the A100 GPU, Dadu-Corki, LUT-DLA, CodeGEMM, and ShiftAddLLM, respectively, with negligible accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [63] [From Proprietary to Open-Source: Bridging the Distribution Gap via Multi-Agent Protocol Distillation in Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24280)
*Junlin Liu,Jiangwang Chen,Zixin Song,Shuaiyu Zhou,Chunji Lv,Hank Wu,Kailin Jiang,Jinyang Wu,Bohan Yu,Chenxi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体协议蒸馏（MAPD）框架，通过结构化的JSON协议作为中间表示，联合知识蒸馏与强化学习，解决异构蒸馏问题，在7个QA基准上显著提升小型语言模型的搜索推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果强化学习为智能搜索提供稀疏监督，知识蒸馏可提供更密集指导，但专有模型的logit匹配因隐藏logit和分词器不匹配不可行，直接模仿自然语言轨迹会传递风格伪影而非推理能力。需解决异构蒸馏问题并弥合分布差距。

Method: 提出MAPD框架：离线多智能体系统将查询分解、检索证据、修复失败搜索，将探索轨迹转换为JSON协议（含任务类型、推理计划、提取性事实）。训练时协议仅提供给学生策略的特权分支，其token分布提供密集蒸馏信号，与稀疏RL目标联合优化。

Result: 在7个QA基准上，MAPD在Qwen3-1.7B上平均成功率39.4%，Qwen3-4B上44.4%，持续优于对比的蒸馏和RL方法。框架对不同专有教师模型泛化鲁棒，有效缓解风格漂移和冗长退化。

Conclusion: MAPD通过结构化协议蒸馏有效弥合了异构教师与学生之间的分布差距，联合蒸馏与RL信号实现了更强的智能搜索推理能力，且具有良好的泛化性和抗退化性。

Abstract: Agentic search enables large language models to solve knowledge-intensive tasks by interleaving multi-step reasoning with retrieval, yet optimizing this with outcome-based reinforcement learning (RL) provides only sparse supervision. Knowledge distillation can supply denser guidance, and advanced proprietary models with their strong reasoning capabilities are promising teachers. While distilling from proprietary models can densify this supervisory signal, conventional logit-matching is precluded by hidden logits and mismatched tokenizers, whereas raw natural language trajectory imitation transfers superficial stylistic artifacts rather than core reasoning competence. To address the heterogeneous distillation problem and bridge the distribution gap, we propose Multi-Agent Protocol Distillation (MAPD), a joint distillation and RL framework uses a structured, style-normalized protocol as an intermediate representation. An offline multi-agent system (MAS) decomposes each query, retrieves supporting evidence, repairs failed searches, and converts the resulting exploration trace into a JSON protocol containing the task type, reasoning plan, and extractive grounding facts. During training, the protocol is provided only to a privileged branch of the student policy, whose token distributions furnish a dense distillation signal alongside the sparse RL objective. Extensive evaluations across seven QA benchmarks demonstrate that MAPD consistently outperforms competitive distillation and RL, achieving average success rates of 39.4\% on Qwen3-1.7B and 44.4\% on Qwen3-4B. Crucially, the framework generalizes robustly across diverse proprietary teachers while effectively mitigating the student policy from style drift and verbosity degeneration.

</details>


### [64] [Unequal Trips, Unequal Places: Diagnosing and Mitigating Delay Inequity in Autonomous Vehicle Fleet Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24336)
*Nicole Hu,Mingtao Zhang,Haoyang LI,Chen Jason Zhang,Li Qing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 城市规模的自动驾驶车队协调通常优化平均出行时间，但本文通过三个真实城市的道路网络和出租车需求数据进行分配审计，发现普遍的行程长度不平等现象。为此提出SPARE（空间感知重路由）框架，通过预算有限的在线协调，将有限的重规划能力分配给延迟车辆，并利用观察到的等待压力进行重定向，在保持城市规模可扩展性的同时实现了最强的效率-公平联合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市级自动驾驶车队协调系统通常以平均出行时间为优化目标，但平均值掩盖了延迟在不同行程和区域之间的分布差异，缺乏对出行公平性的关注。

Method: 首先对曼哈顿、芝加哥和旧金山三个真实城市的道路网络和出租车需求数据进行分配审计，揭示行程长度不平等现象。然后提出SPARE框架，这是一个预算有限的在线协调框架，将有限的重规划能力分配给延迟车辆，并利用近期观察到的等待压力进行重定向，提供每次决策保证并明确限制在线路线更新次数。

Result: 在三个数据集上对六种代表性基线方法的实验表明，SPARE在保持城市规模可扩展性的同时，实现了最强的效率-公平联合性能。结果表明有界的拥塞响应式重路由可以在无需全车队重规划的情况下改善性能和公平性。

Conclusion: 有界的拥塞响应式重路由能在无需全车队重规划的情况下有效改善城市自动驾驶车队的运行效率和公平性，为城市交通管理提供了可扩展的公平性解决方案。

Abstract: City-scale autonomous vehicle fleet coordinators are typically optimized for aggregate travel time, yet fleet averages conceal how delay is distributed across trips and regions. We conduct a distributional audit on three real-city road-network and taxi-demand datasets from Manhattan, Chicago, and San Francisco. The audit reveals pervasive trip-length inequity whose direction depends on the city and coordinator. After accounting for trip length, spatial inequity becomes more pronounced as demand grows and is consistently stronger when trips are grouped by origin rather than destination. These findings motivate SPatially Aware RErouting (SPARE), a budgeted online coordination framework that assigns limited replanning capacity to delayed vehicles and redirects them using recently observed waiting pressure. SPARE provides a per-review decision guarantee and explicitly bounds online route updates. Experiments on all three datasets against six representative baselines show that SPARE delivers the strongest joint efficiency-fairness performance while retaining city-scale scalability. The results demonstrate that bounded congestion-responsive rerouting improves performance and equity without full-fleet replanning.

</details>


### [65] [Simulating Tenant Responses to Energy Policy Interventions with Transaction-Cost-Aware LLM Age](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24341)
*Weijie Xia,Stefanie Horian,Hanyue Huang,Queena K. Qian,Jie Yang,Pedro P. Vergara Barrios*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于感知交易成本（PTC）的摩擦感知人格建模方法，用于LLM模拟人类对政策干预的反应。在荷兰1,068名公民的能源节约改造调查数据上，通过GPT-3.5-turbo、Ministral-8B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型验证，表明PTC人格设计和推理在提示学习和微调设置中均能持续提升模型性能，弥合了制度政策理论与可解释的LLM政策模拟之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM模拟人类意见的方法通常基于人口统计、态度或人格描述，但忽视了影响政策响应的实际、认知或社会摩擦（如信息负担、行政成本、协调需求和感知不确定性）。作者希望引入感知交易成本（PTC）这一理论视角，建模政策响应中的实际摩擦，从而提升LLM模拟的准确性。

Method: 提出基于PTC的摩擦感知人格建模方法。在能源节约改造（EER）场景中，不仅用人口统计信息表示租户，还建模其对成本、收益、障碍和不确定性的感知。使用荷兰1,068名公民的约40,548个问答对数据，比较提示学习（prompt-only）和微调（fine-tuned）两种设置，在GPT-3.5-turbo、Ministral-8B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct三个模型上进行实验，并对本地开源模型采用SFT和GRPO两种微调策略。

Result: 在提示学习和微调两种设置中，引入PTC人格和推理均持续提升了所有模型的性能。

Conclusion: 基于PTC的人格设计有效连接了制度政策理论与可解释的LLM政策模拟，为更真实地模拟人类对政策干预的响应提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent studies use Large language models (LLMs) to simulate human opinions and decisions by prompting models with demographic, attitudinal, or persona-based descriptions. Yet such simulations rarely model the practical, cognitive, or social frictions that shape how people respond to policy interventions. Perceived transaction cost (PTC) provides a useful lens for modeling the practical frictions that shape policy responses, such as information burden, administrative effort, coordination demands, and perceived uncertainty. We use this lens to develop a friction-aware persona modeling approach for LLM-based simulation. In the context of energy-efficient renovation (EER), tenants are represented not only by who they are demographically, but by how they perceive the costs, benefits, barriers, and uncertainties associated with proposed renovation plans. Using survey data collected from 1,068 citizens in the Netherlands, comprising approximately 40,548 survey question and answer pairs, we compare prompt-only and fine-tuned settings across GPT-3.5-turbo, Ministral-8B-Instruct, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and evaluate supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for local open-weight models. Results show that incorporating PTC-based personas and reasoning consistently improves model performance across both prompt-only and fine-tuned settings, suggesting that PTC-based persona design provides a useful bridge between institutional policy theory and interpretable LLM-based policy simulation. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaweijie1996/socialagent.

</details>

### [66] [A Replay-Constrained Simulation Framework for Personalization of Powered Knee--Ankle Prosthesis Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22858)
*Duong Le,Ryan Posh,Shihao Cheng,Maani Ghaffari,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于重放约束的仿真框架，通过MuJoCo模拟器结合记录的人体髋部运动学和地面反作用力数据，实现了对动力假肢膝关节和踝关节阻抗控制器的高维个性化优化，并在三名大腿截肢者实验中验证了其预测有效性（Pearson r=0.96-0.997），学习控制器相比基线提升了42-59%的生物仿射奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力假肢的阻抗控制器个性化对适应个体步态生物力学至关重要，但现有方法依赖耗时的人机交互探索或局限于低维单关节参数空间，缺乏高效可扩展的高维个性化方案。

Method: 构建MuJoCo仿真器，利用记录的人体髋部运动学数据和地面反作用力反馈驱动假肢膝盖-踝关节动力学，绕过对人体神经肌肉控制机制的建模需求；采用深度强化学习策略同时个性化两个关节的相位相关刚度、阻尼和平衡角度，优化基于生物仿射的奖励函数。

Result: 三名大腿截肢者0.8m/s平地行走实验表明，仿真与硬件间预测效度高（Pearson r=0.96-0.997）；最优硬件策略始终落在仿真前五策略内；学习控制器使生物仿射奖励提升42-59%。

Conclusion: 该框架支持动力假肢的可扩展高维个性化，且可扩展至更高维度的控制器参数化（如神经网络控制器）。

Abstract: Personalization of impedance controllers for powered prosthetic legs is critical to accommodating individual gait biomechanics but remains challenging. Existing methods rely on time-intensive human-in-the-loop exploration and/or constrain optimization to low-dimensional, single-joint parameter subspaces. Sim-to-real transfer has enabled high-dimensional locomotion control for legged robots, but in assistive device control the human partner remains un-modelable. We present a replay-constrained simulation framework: a MuJoCo-based simulator reproduces prosthetic knee-ankle dynamics while replaying recorded hip kinematics and feedback-based ground reaction forces from individual walking data, bypassing the need to model complex human neuromuscular control mechanisms. We demonstrate the framework with a deep reinforcement learning policy that personalizes phase-dependent stiffness, damping, and equilibrium angle at both joints simultaneously, maximizing a biomimicry-based reward computed solely from onboard prosthesis measurements. Experiments with three participants with transfemoral amputation during level-ground walking at 0.8~m/s demonstrate strong simulation-to-hardware predictive validity (Pearson $r=0.96$--$0.997$). The best-performing policy on hardware was consistently predicted within the top five simulation policies for all participants. The learned controllers improved overall biomimicry rewards by 42--59\% relative to the unpersonalized baseline. The framework supports scalable high-dimensional personalization of powered prosthetic legs and is amenable to extension to higher-dimensional controller parameterizations such as neural-network controllers.

</details>


### [67] [Pose-Aware Modeling to Mitigate Pose-Related Artifacts in Tactile Gloves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22964)
*Tianhong Catherine Yu,Ziyi Kou,Mia Huang,Taylor Niehues,Yiyue Luo,Li Guan,Dingtian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与手套无关的算法框架，利用手部姿态信息来减轻触觉手套中由姿态变化引起的伪影（PRAs），在不修改硬件的情况下将最小可检测力（MDF）降低了10.4%-18.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉手套中的柔性传感器对手部姿态变化敏感，导致姿态相关伪影（PRAs），尤其在低力范围内造成接触误检或延迟检测，提高了手套的最小可检测力（MDF），影响其在灵巧操作、遥操作和示教学习等机器人应用中的有效性。

Method: 首先表征了PRAs与姿态和力的关系；然后提出了一种与手套无关的算法框架，在触觉到力管道中增加一个残差预测分支，显式利用手部姿态信息来补偿姿态引起的传感器变形。

Result: 在3种手套设计和15名用户上验证，MDF分别降低了10.4%、12.2%和18.3%，所有评估指标均有一致改善。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一条实用的途径来提升触觉手套在数据收集和多样化机器人应用中的可用性，无需修改硬件即可有效缓解姿态伪影问题。

Abstract: Tactile gloves digitize contact and force during hand-object interactions, enabling robotics applications in dexterous manipulation, teleoperation, and learning from demonstration. To preserve hand dexterity and capture the nuances of natural interactions, these gloves and the integrated tactile sensors are designed to be soft, flexible, and comfortable. However, such flexible sensors are sensitive not only to contact forces but also unavoidably to hand pose changes, resulting in pose-related artifacts (PRAs). PRAs are especially problematic in the low-force range, resulting in misdetections or late-onset detections of contact, which raises the minimum detectable force (MDF) of the glove. In this work, we characterize the PRAs in relation to pose and force. Building on these insights, we introduce a glove-agnostic algorithmic framework that leverages hand pose information, which is increasingly available, to mitigate PRAs without glove modifications. Our pose-aware force estimation model augments tactile-to-force pipelines with a residual prediction branch that explicitly accounts for pose-induced sensor deformations.
