Motivation: 扩散策略在机器人操作任务中表现优异，但传统的最小化去噪目标在有限数据（机器人领域常见）下对泛化能力的控制有限。

Method: 将扩散策略建模为贝叶斯神经网络，定义PAC-Bayes泛化界，推导出包含标准去噪损失和后验与先验参数分布之间KL散度正则项的新训练目标。

Result: 在多个机器人操作基准上，PAC-DP表现出更好的去噪性能、更低的变分负对数似然以及更高的任务成功率；在低数据训练场景和复杂任务中提升最为显著。

Conclusion: PAC-DP为机器人策略学习提供了理论上有依据的框架，在不显著增加训练时间的情况下，通过原则性的正则化方法提升了扩散策略的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion Policies (DPs) are able to perform complex manipulation tasks. However, DPs are typically trained by minimizing a denoising objective, which provides limited control over generalization in the finite-data regimes common in robotics. In this letter, we propose PAC-DP, an approach that increases the performance of DPs in robotic manipulation tasks. By modeling the DP as a Bayesian neural network, and defining a PAC-Bayes generalization bound, we derive a novel training objective that augments the standard denoising loss with a Kullback-Leibler divergence regularizer between the posterior and prior parameter distributions. From the theoretical perspective, our approach provides a principled approach to regularize the training of DPs without significantly increasing the training time. From the practical point of view, experimental results demonstrate improved denoising performance, lower variational negative log-likelihood, and higher success rates across multiple robotic manipulation benchmarks. Crucially, the largest improvements are observed in low-data training regimes and complex tasks, establishing PAC-DP as a theoretically grounded framework for robot policy learning.

</details>


### [104] [A note on the motion representation and configuration update in time stepping schemes for the constrained rigid body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24317)
*A. Müller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角研究约束刚体动力学中配置空间的选择对约束满足的影响，证明若约束定义配置空间的子群则数值积分可精确满足约束，并指出SE(3)是数值DAE建模的合适配置空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体系统动力学中，约束刚体运动通常被建模为指标1的微分代数方程系统。实践中常用SO(3)×R³而非SE(3)作为配置空间，但观察发现约束满足程度依赖于配置空间的选择。需要从几何角度解释并确定合适的配置空间。

Method: 采用几何视角分析，研究约束条件定义配置空间子群时的数值积分性质，对比SO(3)×R³与SE(3)作为配置空间对约束满足的影响，并探讨SE(3)子群与机械系统建模（如低副运动副）的关联。

Result: 证明了当约束定义配置空间的子群时，数值积分方案可精确满足约束。SE(3)的子群对机械系统建模具有重要意义，其子群隐含地用于多体系统建模中。

Conclusion: SE(3)是约束刚体数值DAE建模的合适配置空间，但该结论不直接适用于多体系统。

Abstract: The dynamics of a holonomically constrained rigid body can be modeled by Newton-Euler equations subjected to geometric constraints. This is frequently formulated as a differential-algebraic equation (DAE) system of index 1. Inmultibody system (MBS) dynamics it is common (1) to numerically solve this system by means of integration schemes for ordinary differential equations, and (2) to treat the rigid body motion on the direct product Lie group SO (3)R3, although rigid body motions form the semidirect product Lie group SE (3). It is has been observed that the constraint satisfaction depends on which Lie group is used as configuration space (c-space). In this paper the problem is considered from a geometric perspective. It is shown that the constraints are exactly satisfied by a numerical integration scheme if they define a subgroup of the c-space. The subgroups of SE (3) have a significance for modeling mechanical systems, including lower kinematic (Reuleaux) pairs and are implicitly used in MBS modeling. It is concluded that SE (3) is the appropriate cspace for numerical DAE modeling of a constrained rigid body. This result does not immediately apply to MBS, however.

</details>


### [105] [Continual-RL for Generalization in Autonomous Racing on the RoboRacer Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24320)
*Joel Siegert,Edoardo Ghignone,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Continual Backpropagation的持续强化学习框架，使自动驾驶赛车能在真实世界中仅用15分钟微调策略，超越经典控制器，并对比了离线RL方法，分析了塑性特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人需要适应不断变化的环境，但仿真无法覆盖所有真实场景，因此需要在物理世界进行强化学习。持续强化学习虽能解决此问题，但其框架和方法尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出基于Continual Backpropagation的持续RL框架，先在多个赛道上训练通用策略，然后在真实世界中15分钟内微调；并设计了基于离线RL的对比方法，进行了塑性特性的仿真分析。

Result: 该框架仅使用真实数据即可训练通用策略，并在15分钟微调后超越经典控制器；离线RL对比方法提供了性能基线。

Conclusion: 持续反向传播框架能有效解决真实世界中的持续学习问题，为自动驾驶赛车等机器人领域提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A key challenge in modern robotics is to adapt to changing environments, a challenge that is exacerbated when simulations cannot encompass every possible real-world configuration, and therefore Reinforcement Learning (RL) in the physical world becomes necessary. Continual Reinforcement Learning provides the tools to address this challenge; however, both the frameworks and the methods remain underexplored. Autonomous Racing and in particular the RoboRacer competition provide a testing ground for such methods, as learning to drive on a new track-floor combination with the least amount of new experience naturally frames a continual learning problem. This work tries to address this gap by proposing a continual RL framework based on Continual Backpropagation that is able, with only real-world data, to train a generalistic policy on a set of tracks and then fine- tune it within 15 minutes to outperform classical controllers. Furthermore, a comparison method based on offline RL is proposed, and a simulation analysis of the plasticity properties of the methods is conducted.

</details>


### [106] [Model Predictive Planner for UAV Navigation in Non-Convex Air Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24369)
*Henrique Silva,Marcelo A. Santos,Guilherme V. Raffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合整数跟踪模型预测控制的无人机运动规划框架，通过在单一优化问题中嵌入最短路径偏移成本和可行性约束，解决了非凸城市空中走廊中的导航问题，无需外部全局规划即可生成满足动态和走廊约束的收敛轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机导航方案在处理非凸城市空中走廊时，容易陷入局部极小值，且通常需要依赖外部全局规划阶段，导致系统复杂度和计算负担增加。

Method: 采用混合整数跟踪模型预测控制（MI-TMPC）作为核心公式，将走廊可行性和动态一致性约束纳入同一优化问题；通过引入基于最短路径的偏移成本和可行性约束，直接在规划层解决非凸几何引起的局部极小值问题。

Result: 数值仿真结果表明，该方法能够生成满足走廊约束的动态可行轨迹，并且无需外部全局规划阶段即可实现向目标的收敛。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够在不依赖外部全局规划器的情况下有效解决非凸城市空中走廊中的无人机导航问题，验证了MI-TMPC结合最短路径偏移策略的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: This work presents a motion planning framework for UAV navigation in non-convex urban air corridors. The planner is based on a mixed-integer tracking model predictive control formulation that enforces corridor feasibility and dynamic consistency within a single optimization problem. To guarantee convergence to the target and mitigate the occurrence of local minima induced by non-convex geometry, a shortest-path-based offset cost with feasibility constraints is embedded directly into the planning problem. Numerical simulations show that the proposed formulation generates dynamically valid trajectories that satisfy the corridor constraints and converge to the target without relying on external global planning stages.

</details>


### [107] [ArmnetBench v0.1: Parallel Real-World Evaluation of Manipulation Policies on a Low-Cost Arm Farm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24481)
*Praveen Selvaraj,Lorenzo Uttini,Ville Kuosmanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ArmnetBench v0.1，一个基于低成本SO-101机械臂集群的机器人操作策略评估基准，包含7种策略在12个任务上的2,518次策略部署和600次参考演示，所有3,118个轨迹均带有三分类标签（成功/次优/失败），支持下游学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界评估是通用机器人操作策略开发中的瓶颈，每次部署都需要物理硬件和操作员进行设置、重置和评分，成本高、效率低。

Method: 使用低成本的SO-101机械臂组建集群（arm farm），在轻量现场监督下运行基准测试，对7种策略在12个任务上进行评估，包括单臂和双臂配置，每种策略在每任务上使用50个演示进行训练或微调。

Result: 收集了2,518次策略部署和600次参考演示（共3,118个轨迹），所有轨迹都带有人工标注的三分类标签，并发布了leaderboard作为共享预算下的初始比较。

Conclusion: 该基准不仅能评估策略，其质量标注的轨迹数据还能支持下游学习任务（如奖励建模、预测世界模型、混合质量数据训练等），并开源了3,118个核心轨迹数据。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation is a bottleneck in developing generalist robot manipulation policies. Each rollout requires physical hardware and an operator to set up, reset, and score it. We introduce ArmnetBench v0.1, a benchmark run on a fleet of low-cost SO-101 cells under light on-site supervision. v0.1 validates this arm farm end to end and compares 7 policies across 12 tasks with both single-arm and bimanual configurations. Each policy is trained or fine-tuned on 50 demonstrations per task; the benchmark contains 2,518 policy rollouts and 600 reference demonstrations. All 3,118 episodes carry a three-way label (successful, suboptimal, or failure). Policy rollouts are human-scored, while demonstrations are successful by construction. Beyond evaluation, its quality-labelled trajectories support downstream learning, from reward and predictive world models to policies trained on mixed-quality data. The leaderboard is an initial comparison under this shared budget. We release the 3,118 core episodes in LeRobot v3.0 and RoboMeter formats.

</details>


### [108] [Distributed Coordination for Resilient Multi-UAV Remote Sensing: A Photovoltaic Inspection Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24482)
*Guillermo GP-Lenza,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Martin Molina,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwarmLink是一种无需修改主机系统的无人机群去中心化通信基础设施，通过分布式拍卖机制统一处理初始任务分配、平台故障恢复和数据质量触发的重新分配，解决了集中式地面站单点故障问题，并在光伏电站巡检场景中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机遥感任务中，集中式地面站协调存在单点故障风险（通信中断或地面站故障会导致感知缺口，需要人工干预），而现有框架缺乏非侵入式的去中心化协同能力。

Method: 提出SwarmLink通信基础设施，通过非侵入方式扩展现有空中框架（如Aerostack2）以实现点对点协调能力；设计分布式拍卖机制，将初始目标分配、平台故障恢复和数据质量触发重分配统一为同一运行时机制。

Result: 三种中断场景（初始分配、平台故障、数据质量问题）均归约为对剩余目标和活跃平台的重新拍卖，无需修改Aerostack2核心代码，也无需地面站参与。

Conclusion: SwarmLink通过去中心化拍卖机制有效解决了集中式协调的单点故障问题，实现了零修改、零地面站依赖的无人机群自主协同，适用于大规模遥感巡检任务。

Abstract: Deploying multiple UAVs for remote sensing enables proportional reductions in mission time, but realizing these benefits requires the fleet to coordinate at runtime: distributing sensing targets, responding to platform failures, and recovering from degraded data quality. In inspection campaigns, where mission value depends on complete coverage and the usability of every capture, a centralized ground-station coordinator is a single point of failure: a lost link or station fault leaves sensing gaps that cannot be filled without operator intervention. We propose the \textbf{SwarmLink}, an inter-agent communication infrastructure that non-invasively extends any existing aerial framework with peer-to-peer coordination capability, without modifying the host system. We apply it to photovoltaic plant inspection as a representative large-scale sensing campaign, extending Aerostack2 with a distributed auction that unifies initial sensing-target allocation, platform-failure recovery, and data-quality-triggered reassignment into a single runtime mechanism. All three disruption scenarios reduce to the same re-auction over remaining targets and active platforms, requiring zero modifications to the Aerostack2 core and no ground-station involvement during the mission.

</details>


### [109] [τ: Learning Touch-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Models from Future Visual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24485)
*Ning Cheng,Jinan Xu,Wanlin Li,Yangzhi Chen,Jing Gao,Yiqun Wang,Kelan Peng,Wenjuan Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出τ框架，通过联合嵌入预测架构从未来视觉监督中学习动作条件化的时空触觉表征，并将其与视觉-语言特征融合，在数据有限的情况下提升机器人精细操作性能。同时引入TacAura数据集，实验表明该方法在泛化性和鲁棒性上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将触觉表征适配到预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型时面临双重挑战：数据层面，任务专用演示有限制约表征质量，大规模预训练成本高昂；建模层面，现有方法或仅关注瞬时接触状态，或使用6D力/力矩序列建模时间交互动态，高维触觉信号未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出τ框架，核心创新包括：1）受联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）启发，利用未来视觉监督在隐空间中学习动作条件化的时空触觉表征，该监督仅在训练阶段使用，不增加部署开销；2）将所学触觉表征与视觉-语言特征融合进行动作生成；3）构建TacAura数据集，包含四个代表性接触丰富操作任务的同步视觉、本体感知和基于视觉的触觉信号。

Result: 实验表明，τ框架在接触丰富操作任务中优于现有模型，能够泛化到未见过的物体和场景，展现出更优的操作性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过未来视觉监督引导触觉表征学习并融合多模态信息，是提升有限数据下VLA模型操作能力的有效途径，为触觉-视觉-语言融合的机器人学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Learning the informative tactile representation while effectively adapting it to pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains challenging at both the data and modeling levels. At the data level, limited task-specific demonstrations constrain representation quality, whereas large-scale pretraining incurs substantial costs. At the modeling level, existing methods either focus on instantaneous contact states or model temporal interaction dynamics using 6D wrench sequences, leaving high-dimensional tactile signals underexplored. To address these challenges, we present τ, a touch-augmented VLA framework that learns an action-conditioned spatiotemporal tactile representation from future visual supervision inspired by the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), and fuses it with vision-language features for action generation under limited data. This supervision operates in latent space and is used only during training, adding no deployment overhead. We also introduce TacAura, a dataset of synchronized vision, proprioception, and vision-based tactile signals across four representative contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experiments show that τ outperforms existing models and generalizes to unseen objects and scenes, delivering improved manipulation performance and robustness

</details>


### [110] [KAI: A Kinematic-Aware Interface for Data-Efficient Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24493)
*Yaping Li,Zhaxizhuoma,Qiaojun Yu,Jia Zeng,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为KAI的结构化中间表示，通过嵌入可解释的几何和运动学先验，显著提升了关节物体操作策略的样本效率和泛化能力，在仅使用一半演示数据的情况下达到82.9%的平均成功率，并可通过人类交互视频协同训练进一步增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关节物体操作需要理解运动学结构，但仅从机器人演示中学习这一结构既困难又昂贵，因此需要一种更高效的表示方法。

Method: 提出Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface (KAI)，一种捕获关节物体运动学结构的结构化中间表示，将可解释的几何和运动学先验嵌入到策略学习中，提供与关节运动结构对齐的强归纳偏置。

Result: 在六个仿真任务中，使用一半演示数据达到82.9%的平均成功率，匹配或超越基线性能；能泛化到未见背景和视觉干扰物；结合人类交互视频协同训练，在多种视觉干扰下达到超过70%的平均成功率。

Conclusion: KAI设计有效提升了样本效率（尤其在低数据场景下），具备良好的泛化能力和跨域迁移能力，且动作无关的设计使其能够利用人类交互视频进行协同训练。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation requires an understanding of kinematic structure that is difficult and costly to learn from robot demonstrations alone. We introduce the Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface (KAI), a structured intermediate representation that captures the kinematic structure of articulated objects. By embedding interpretable geometric and kinematic priors into policy learning, KAI provides a strong inductive bias aligned with the underlying structure of articulated motion. This design effectively improves sample efficiency, with gains particularly pronounced in low-data regimes: across six simulation tasks, our method achieves an average success rate of 82.9%, matching or surpassing baseline performance while using only half the demonstration data. Our method also exhibits robust generalization to unseen backgrounds and visual distractors, transferring from a single clean training environment to cluttered real-world scenes. KAI's action-agnostic design further enables co-training with human interaction videos to enhance real-world robustness: under diverse visual distractions, our method with video co-training achieves over 70% average success rate.

</details>


### [111] [NEO: NeRF It Once, Edit It Many Times for Continuous Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24538)
*Mikołaj Zieliński,David Hall,Dominik Belter,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEO是一个统一的语言引导NeRF编辑框架，用于机器人操作，包含语言引导的对象移除、基于知识蒸馏的NeRF权重编辑方法，以及首个用于评估机器人操作场景NeRF编辑的基准数据集NEO-Dataset。该方法在对象移除和拾取-放置机器人实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NeRF编辑方法在机器人操作场景中存在视觉不一致、几何不连贯以及伪影等问题，缺乏统一的框架来支持语言引导的场景编辑以服务于机器人操作任务。

Method: (i) 语言引导的对象移除：结合神经场重采样与多视图一致的渐进式修复；(ii) 直接NeRF权重编辑：利用知识蒸馏技术，通过教师-学生模型组合原始NeRF与编辑后的NeRF，实现机器人执行动作前对未来场景状态的连贯建模；(iii) 构建首个用于定量评估机器人操作场景NeRF编辑方法的基准数据集NEO-Dataset。

Result: 在场景编辑任务（对象移除和拾取-放置机器人实验）中，NEO优于最先进的基线方法，生成的编辑在视觉和几何上更连贯，减少了先前方法常见的伪影。

Conclusion: NEO框架通过语言引导的NeRF编辑有效提升了机器人操作场景的编辑质量，为机器人操作中的场景理解与规划提供了统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present NEO, a unified framework providing language-guided NeRF editing for robotic manipulation. Our paper introduces (i) a language-guided object removal that combines neural field resampling with multiview-consistent progressive inpainting, (ii) a direct NeRF weight editing method utilizing knowledge distillation, composing original and edited NeRFs via a teacher-student model, enabling coherent modeling of future scene states before a robot executes an action, and (iii) the first benchmark (NEO-Dataset) for quantitatively evaluating NeRF scene editing methods suitable for robot manipulation. We show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in scene editing tasks, including object removal and pick-and-place robotic experiments, yielding visually coherent and geometrically consistent edits that reduce artifacts commonly introduced by prior methods.

</details>


### [112] [Development of a Handheld Actuation Mechanism for a Tendon-driven Robotically Steered Guidewire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24629)
*Saima Chavenet,Timothy A. Brumfiel,Revanth Konda,Jaydev P. Desai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种手持式腱驱动机器人导丝驱动机构，通过紧凑的卷轴机制实现电机驱动的导丝转向以及手动旋转和推进。该设备允许临床医生通过手柄上的摇杆和开关控制长达1.5米的导丝进行推进、旋转和弯曲运动，并在解剖学精确的主动脉模型中验证了导航效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动导丝导航面临血管迂曲、导丝尖端缺乏可控性等挑战，可能导致血管损伤、穿孔、夹层、闭塞及术后并发症（如血栓形成），因此需要开发更安全、易操作的导丝驱动装置。

Method: 将紧凑的卷轴机构集成到手持设备中，采用腱驱动机器人导丝，通过手柄上的摇杆和瞬时开关控制电机驱动，实现导丝的推进、旋转和弯曲运动，同时保留手动操作能力。

Result: 成功开发了手持式驱动装置，可控制长达1.5米的机器人可转向导丝，并在解剖学精确的主动脉模型实验中验证了导航能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的手持式驱动装置结合了电机驱动转向与手动操作能力，为血管介入手术中的导丝导航提供了一种更安全、可控的解决方案。

Abstract: An endovascular intervention begins with a skilled clinician manually navigating a long, slender wire, called a guidewire, to the target location within the vasculature. Due to factors, such as vessel tortuosity and lack of steerability at the guidewire tip, manual navigation of a guidewire could be challenging, potentially resulting in vessel damage, perforation, dissection, and occlusion, as well as postsurgical complications, such as thrombosis. This work details the development of a handheld actuation mechanism for tendon-driven robotic guidewires by utilizing the aforementioned spooling mechanism. Incorporating the compact spooling mechanism into a handheld device enables clinicians to leverage both motorized guidewire steering and manual rotation and translation, if desired. The proposed device is designed such that, while holding the device, the clinician can execute feeding, rotation, and bending motions for up to 1.5 m of the robotically steerable guidewire using a joystick and momentary switch on the handle. Furthermore, the proposed device is demonstrated through navigation in an anatomically accurate phantom aorta model.

</details>


### [113] [Data Pyramid for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24744)
*Yifan Ye,Yankai Fu,Yaoxu Lv,Bohan Hou,Jun Cen,Lingdong Kong,Duo Zheng,Tianxing Chen,Jiaming Liu,Ziang Cao,Yunfan Lou,Wei Chow,Xian Sun,Yingshuo Wang,Kuangzhi Ge,Xiaowei Chi,Xidong Zhang,Zhibo Pang,Yiwu Zhong,Sirui Han,Zhihe Lu,Weihao Yuan,Qifeng Chen,Michael Yu Wang,Yao Mu,Ziwei Liu,Jianfei Yang,Ping Luo,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了具身智能体数据生态，提出“数据金字塔”框架，将数据源按可扩展性与机器人对齐的张力分为五类，并分析了不同数据配方对具身基础模型能力的影响，最后指出了六大开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体缺乏类似多模态基础模型那种直接从互联网海量数据学习的捷径，需要耦合观测、物理状态和动作的数据。现有数据来源多样但缺乏系统性组织，亟需一个统一框架来理解不同数据源的特点及其对模型能力的影响。

Method: 提出“数据金字塔”框架，将具身数据源按可扩展性与机器人对齐的张力分为五类：真实机器人数据、UMI式数据、自我中心与外部中心数据、仿真数据、通用视觉-语言数据。从数据质量、多样性、可重用性和物理保真度四个维度刻画每种数据源，并结合近期具身基础模型的数据配方进行分析。

Result: 明确了不同数据源在感知、推理、规划、动作生成和世界预测等能力上的贡献差异；揭示了具身脑模型、视觉-语言-动作模型和世界-动作模型在数据选择、对齐和混合策略上的不同模式。

Conclusion: 指出六大开放挑战：大规模触觉数据集构建、失败与恢复数据收集、可扩展数据采集管线开发、跨本体动作对齐、自我中心数据在灵巧操作中的应用、以及设计有原则的机器人学习数据配方，为下一代具身智能系统设计奠定基础。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models learned to see and to speak by consuming the whole internet. Embodied agents admit no such shortcut, since they require data that couple observations with physical states and actions. These signals can be provided, to varying degrees, by multiple data sources. In this work, we organize the embodied data ecosystem as a "pyramid" spanning five complementary sources: real-robot data, UMI-style data, egocentric and exocentric data, simulation data, and general vision-language data. We organize the pyramid around the tension between scalability and robot alignment, and further characterize each source in terms of data quality, diversity, reusability, and physical fidelity. We then analyze recent embodied foundation models through the lens of their data recipes, examining how different sources are selected, aligned, and mixed during pretraining. For embodied brain models, vision-language-action models, and world-action models alike, we relate data composition to capabilities in perception, reasoning, planning, action generation, and world prediction. We close by discussing six open challenges: building large-scale tactile datasets, collecting failure and recovery data, developing scalable data-collection pipelines, aligning actions across embodiments, leveraging egocentric data for dexterous manipulation, and designing principled data recipes for robot learning. We hope this work paves the foundation for the design of next-generation embodied systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22724)
*Kaibing Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Shengtian Yang,Shuo He,Yu Li,Mengyi Liu,Pengwei Chen,Jun Xu,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对组策略优化在长周期任务中因采样不平衡导致的信用分配困境（重复低效动作主导、有用动作采样不足），提出ProGPO方法，在零奖励组内基于首次访问状态覆盖度分配优势权重，在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上显著提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组策略优化在长周期任务中存在采样不平衡问题：重复或低效动作占据高概率区域，而能改变状态的有用动作采样不足，导致大量全失败组，此时结果奖励无法提供梯度校正方向，形成自我强化的信用陷阱。

Method: ProGPO：当组内所有样本的结果奖励为零时，使用首次访问观测覆盖度（first-visit observation coverage）——即访问更多新状态的轨迹或步骤被赋予更高相对优势，因为到达新观测是任务成功的先决条件。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个困难agentic基准上，基于Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B-Instruct的实验显示，ProGPO一致优于组策略优化基线，在困难任务上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: ProGPO通过打破信用陷阱，有效解决了组策略优化在长周期任务中的采样不平衡问题，验证了零奖励组内基于状态覆盖度的优势分配的有效性。

Abstract: Group-based policy optimization has been increasingly used to train large language model (LLM) agents from sparse outcome rewards by comparing trajectories or steps within a group. However, on difficult long-horizon tasks, this comparison can suffer from a sampling imbalance: repeated or low-effect actions dominate the high-probability region of the policy while useful state-changing actions remain under-sampled. This imbalance produces many all-failed rollout groups, where outcome rewards provide no direction for correcting the policy. Together, these effects can form a self-reinforcing credit trap: failure-dominated sampling yields no outcome-based correction, allowing repeated low-effect actions to persist. To break this loop, we propose Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization (ProGPO), which uses first-visit observation coverage only when all samples in a group receive zero outcome reward. Specifically, within such groups, ProGPO assigns higher relative advantages to trajectories or steps that visit more new states since reaching new observations is a prerequisite for task success. Experiments on two challenging agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5/7B-Instruct, show that ProGPO consistently improves over group-based baselines, with particularly large gains on hard tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Shapley: An Influence-Based Data Auditing Pipeline for LLM Alignment and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22766)
*Yunting Song,Matthew Watson,Peter Grabowski,Jun Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的、仅需推理的数据估值流程，通过近似Shapley值来评估数据质量，无需迭代重新训练模型。该方法将语义k-NN邻域映射为有向图，利用参考LLM的零样本和单样本条件对数似然变化直接评估数据效用，并将预测影响分数转化为局部优势度量以隔离梯度冲突的记录。在HelpSteer2和HH-RLHF数据集上的实验表明，该方法能将人工审计搜索空间减少99.1%，并发现数千个隐藏的安全和事实偏好反转问题，同时暴露了当前基准测试中标签错误的严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的对齐效果越来越受限于数据质量，随着数据集规模扩大，海量偏好和指令微调语料库中不可避免会积累隐藏的结构性矛盾、安全风险和系统性的人工标注错误。现有数据集审计方法（如语义去重或LLM-as-a-judge）难以捕捉单个记录对模型预测的实际影响，且常常遗漏深层次的功能规则冲突。

Method: 提出了一种可扩展的、仅需推理的数据估值流程。核心步骤包括：(1) 将语义k-NN邻域映射为有向图；(2) 通过参考LLM的零样本和单样本条件对数似然变化（zero-shot and one-shot conditional log-likelihood shifts）直接评估数据效用；(3) 将预测影响分数转化为局部优势度量（localized advantage metrics）以隔离梯度冲突记录。该方法近似Shapley值但不需迭代重新训练。

Result: 在HelpSteer2数据集上，该方法将手动审计搜索空间减少99.1%，成功发现不同失败模式下的错误标记记录。在Anthropic的HH-RLHF数据集上，识别出数千个隐藏的安全和事实偏好反转问题。此外，将该审计扩展到评估集后，揭示了当前基准测试的严重漏洞：高能力模型频繁预测更安全或更有帮助的响应，却受到客观上存在缺陷的人工标签的惩罚。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个数学基础扎实、高效的数据集诊断工具，能够揭示人工标签错误、清理评估基准、确保LLM对齐数据的完整性。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly bottlenecked by data quality. As datasets scale, massive preference and instruction-tuning corpora inevitably accumulate hidden structural contradictions, safety risks, and systemic human annotation errors. Standard dataset auditing methods, such as semantic deduplication or LLM-as-a-judge, struggle to capture the actual predictive impact of individual records and often miss deep functional rule clashes. To address this, we introduce a scalable, inference-only data valuation pipeline that approximates the Shapley value without iterative model retraining. By mapping semantic k-NN neighborhoods into a directed graph, our framework evaluates data utility directly through a reference LLM's probability distribution using zero-shot and one-shot conditional log-likelihood shifts. Our pipeline then translates these predictive influence scores into localized advantage metrics to isolate gradient-conflicting records. We demonstrate the pipeline's efficacy in sanitizing two heavily vetted alignment datasets. First, applying our pipeline to the HelpSteer2 dataset reduced the manual audit search space by 99.1%, successfully uncovering falsely-labeled records across diverse failure modes. Second, applying our automated audit strategy to Anthropic's HH-RLHF training and evaluation splits identified thousands of hidden safety and factual preference inversions. Crucially, by extending this audit to the evaluation split, we expose severe vulnerabilities in current benchmark integrity: highly capable models frequently predict the safer or more helpful response, only to be penalized by objectively flawed human ground-truth labels. Overall, our work provides a mathematically grounded, highly efficient diagnostic tool to uncover human label failures, sanitize evaluation benchmarks, and ensure the integrity of LLM alignment data.

</details>


### [116] [MEMENTO: Memory-Guided Memetic Code-as-Policy Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22832)
*Alkis Sygkounas,Victor Aregbede,Amy Loutfi,Andreas Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEMENTO 提出一种记忆引导的单精英模因框架，用于代码即策略的进化优化，通过演化评估器并利用记忆引导的爬山、宏突变和交叉操作改进策略，在长时程具身任务中优于现有基线方法，并成功实现 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的代码即策略进化方法主要从独立生成的变体中选择候选策略，缺乏序贯局部改进阶段，导致策略搜索效率不足。

Method: 提出 MEMENTO 框架，包含：(1) 演化 rollout 评估器，输出标量适应度和结构化反馈指标；(2) 记忆引导的单精英模因进化，通过记忆引导的爬山（局部改进）、宏突变和交叉操作生成策略变体；(3) 适应度用于选择候选和精英，反馈指标用于调节策略生成。

Result: 在 Robosuite Franka Tower-of-Hanoi 操作和 AI2-THOR 家庭交互任务中，MEMENTO 在任务成功率和泛化能力上优于 Eureka 和 REvolve 基线。消融实验表明零样本生成和无进化评估器无法解决任务，去除策略搜索分支会降低性能。物理 Franka 机器人上验证了 sim-to-real 迁移可行性。

Conclusion: 记忆引导的序贯局部改进机制对于代码即策略的进化优化至关重要，结合演化评估器可有效提升长时程具身任务策略的质量和泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks require policies that execute many dependent actions before task success can be observed. Representing policies as executable control pro- grams (code-as-policy) enables their decision logic to be inspected and revised after rollout evaluation. Revised programs can then be executed and compared by rollout performance, framing policy improvement as execution-guided program search. Evo- lutionary methods driven by large language models (LLMs) provide a natural mecha- nism for this search by generating variants and selecting high-performing candidates. However, existing approaches primarily select among independently generated vari- ants and lack a sequential local improvement phase. We introduce MEMENTO, a memory-guided single-elite memetic framework for code-as-policy evolution. ME- MENTO first evolves a rollout evaluator that maps policy rollouts to scalar fitness and structured feedback metrics. Fitness selects accepted candidates and the next elite, while feedback metrics condition policy proposals generated by memory-guided hill-climbing, macro-mutation, and crossover. We evaluate MEMENTO on two long- horizon embodied domains: Robosuite Franka Tower-of-Hanoi manipulation and AI2- THOR household interaction. MEMENTO outperforms Eureka and REvolve, adapted as code-as-policy evolutionary baselines, in task success and generalization to held- out Robosuite object configurations and unseen AI2-THOR scenes. Ablations show that zero-shot generation and unevolved evaluators fail to solve either domain, and that removing policy-search branches reduces performance. Finally, we deploy the best-evolved Robosuite policy on a physical Franka robot, demonstrating the feasibil- ity of sim-to-real transfer of the evolved code-as-policy. Code, prompts, and videos are available at: https://github.com/sygkounas/MEMENTO.

</details>


### [117] [Frustratingly Simple Black-Box Adaptation of Language Models via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22837)
*Ofek I. Cohen,Lior Shani,Aviv Rosenberg,Ankur Samanta,Tal Wagner,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级的语言模型适配方法：通过学习一个固定的logit偏置向量，在解码时对每个token的logits进行偏置，无需修改模型权重或进行梯度计算，即可在数学和推理任务上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在内部使用语言模型时面临领域适配和隐私保护的双重挑战，传统的微调开源模型或提示优化方法操作复杂、成本高，因此需要一种更简单、低权限要求的模型适配方案。

Method: 基于KL正则化的强化学习目标，推导出固定logit偏置向量可以近似最优前缀依赖校正的条件；提出一种黑盒学习方法，利用rollout、奖励和token概率，通过闭式逆概率估计器学习单一、上下文无关的logit偏置向量，在每步解码时施加偏置。

Result: 在数学和推理基准测试上，该方法在仅使用极少量可训练参数的情况下，性能优于基础模型，接近或超过传统微调方法。

Conclusion: 学习得到的logit偏置是一种轻量级的模型适配机制，适用于对模型访问权限最低（仅需API级别控制）的场景，在隐私保护和操作简便性方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Many organizations aim to adapt language models for internal use, both to improve performance on domain-specific tasks and to address privacy concerns around sensitive data. However, such adaptation remains non-trivial: it often requires operationally challenging fine-tuning of open-source models or ad hoc prompt optimization. We study a minimal alternative based on a simple API-level control: allowing users to bias the model's logits with a user-defined vector. We develop a black-box method for learning a single context-independent logit-bias vector, added at every decoding step, without modifying model weights or requiring gradients. Starting from a KL-regularized reinforcement learning (RL) objective, we characterize when such a fixed logit-bias vector can approximate the optimal prefix-dependent correction and derive a closed-form inverse-propensity estimator from rollouts, rewards, and token probabilities. Empirically, this simple decoding-time intervention improves over base models on mathematical and reasoning benchmarks while using far fewer trainable parameters than conventional fine-tuning. Our results suggest that learned logit bias is a lightweight mechanism for adapting language models under minimal access requirements.

</details>


### [118] [Finite-Time Analysis of the Natural Policy Gradient in Finite-Horizon Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22982)
*Asha Barua,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域Markov决策过程中精确自然策略梯度（NPG）算法的收敛性，在已知动态和时变转移核设定下，首次给出了该算法的有限时间收敛保证。常数步长下达到亚线性收敛率$\mathcal{O}(H^{2}/t)$，并推广到线性MDP；递增步长下实现线性收敛率$\mathcal{O}((1-1/\vartheta_\rho)^t)$，其中基于horizon的鲁棒步长调度$η_t=η_0(H/(H-1))^t$同样达到该几何速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPG是TRPO和PPO等成功算法的基础，但其在有限时域且具有时变转移核的设定下缺乏严格的有限时间收敛分析，现有理论结果多针对无限时域或固定转移核场景。

Method: 针对已知动力学和horizon-dependent转移核的有限时域MDP，分别采用常数步长和递增步长两种策略分析精确NPG的收敛性。常数步长分析还扩展到线性MDP（在全支持投影分布下的精确population-projection oracle）。递增步长方面设计了horizon-only鲁棒调度$η_t=η_0(H/(H-1))^t$。

Result: 常数步长下NPG以$\mathcal{O}(H^{2}/t)$速率亚线性收敛，线性MDP中恢复相同率。递增步长下达到线性收敛率$\mathcal{O}((1-1/\vartheta_\rho)^t)$，其中$\vartheta_\rho>1$为问题相关常数；所提鲁棒步长调度同样实现该几何速率。

Conclusion: 首次为有限时域及时变转移核设定下的精确NPG提供了完整的有限时间收敛性分析，填补了该领域的理论空白，并为实际步长选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Natural Policy Gradient (NPG) is a well-established Reinforcement Learning algorithm that underlies widely used methods such as Trust Region Policy Optimization and Proximal Policy Optimization, both of which have demonstrated strong empirical success. In this paper, we study exact NPG in finite-horizon Markov Decision Processes with known dynamics and horizon-dependent transition kernels. We provide the first finite-time convergence guarantees for this algorithm in this setting, for which we consider both constant and increasing step size regimes. With a constant step size $η_t=η$, we prove that NPG converges sublinearly with a rate of $\mathcal{O}(H^{2}/t)$ after $t$ iterations, where $H$ is the horizon length. We also extend this constant step size analysis to linear MDPs in an exact population-projection oracle under a full support projection distribution, recovering the same sublinear rate as in the tabular setting. Furthermore, with increasing step sizes, we prove that this algorithm achieves a linear convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{\vartheta_ρ}\right)^t\right)$ for a problem-dependent constant $\vartheta_ρ> 1$, and the horizon-only robust schedule of the form $η_t=η_0(H/(H-1))^t$ where $η_0>0$ and $H \geq 2$, attains this same geometric rate.

</details>


### [119] [Label-free Industrial Fault Detection via Adversarial Inverse Reinforcement Learning: A System for Run-to-Failure Prognostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22987)
*Dhiraj Neupane,Mohamed Reda Bouadjenek,Richard Dazeley,Sunil Aryal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗逆强化学习（AIRL）框架，将机械故障检测（MFD）建模为离线逆强化学习问题，从观测状态转移中恢复内在"健康"奖励，无需故障标签即可实现跨数据集的非饱和后检测一致性，优于传统监督学习、上下文赌博机（CB）和重建类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MFD方法严重依赖监督学习，但实际场景中故障标签稀缺；而所谓的"基于RL"的MFD方法实质退化为静态上下文赌博机，忽略了状态转移和时间折扣因子，本质上仍是标准监督分类。需要一种无需标签、能捕捉退化动态过程的方法。

Method: 采用对抗逆强化学习（AIRL）框架，将MFD重新定义为离线逆强化学习问题，直接从观测状态转移中恢复内在的"健康"奖励函数，无需手动设计奖励函数或故障标签。

Result: 在HUMS2023、IMS、XJTU-SY三个全寿命至失效基准数据集上，AIRL是唯一在所有数据集上实现非饱和后检测一致性的方法；CB基线无法检测渐变退化，重建类方法崩溃为全异常状态。

Conclusion: AIRL通过逆强化学习有效捕捉退化过程的时序动态特性，无需故障标签即可实现一致的故障检测性能，解决了现有方法在动态建模和标签依赖上的根本缺陷。

Abstract: Machinery fault detection (MFD) remains heavily reliant on supervised learning, which struggles with the scarcity of fault labels in real-world settings. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a framework to model the sequential nature of degradation, current ``RL-based'' MFD methods reduce the problem to a static contextual bandit (CB) formulation: by ignoring state transitions and discarding the temporal discount factor, they collapse to standard supervised classification. We propose an adversarial inverse reinforcement learning (AIRL) framework that treats MFD as an offline IRL problem. Unlike reconstruction-based approaches that rely on static error margins, or CBs that ignore dynamics, our method recovers an intrinsic "health" reward directly from observational state transitions, requiring neither manual reward engineering nor fault labels. On three run-to-failure benchmarks (HUMS2023, IMS, XJTU-SY), AIRL is the only method achieving non-saturated post-detection consistency across all datasets, while CB baselines fail to detect gradual degradation and reconstruction models collapse into always-anomalous states. Code and data: https://github.com/dhirajneupane/AIRL-MFD-DN.

</details>


### [120] [Online Policy Evaluation for MDPs with Dynamic UBSR Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23030)
*Weikai Wang,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于线性函数近似的在线强化学习策略评估算法UBSR-TD，用于处理动态效用型短缺风险度量，通过将损失函数融入时序差分误差，使风险中性MDP的策略评估方法可自然扩展到风险敏感场景，并证明了算法的几乎必然收敛性及多种加速变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有效用型短缺风险（UBSR）度量下的策略评估方法要么局限于限制性强的风险度量类别，要么依赖模拟器访问，无法直接应用于完全在线设置。本文旨在开发计算高效、无需模拟器的在线学习算法。

Method: 提出UBSR-TD算法，将损失函数融入时序差分（TD）误差，使得风险中性MDP的策略评估方法可直接适配到动态UBSR场景；推导了几乎必然收敛的条件；并开发了多种加速收敛的变体。

Result: 数值实验验证了理论结果；在带有保质期不确定性的易腐库存管理问题中展示了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将损失函数引入TD误差，线性函数近似下的在线策略评估可高效扩展到动态UBSR风险度量，为风险感知强化学习提供了实用的算法框架。

Abstract: Developing efficient function-approximation methods for policy evaluation is a fundamental challenge in risk-aware reinforcement learning. Existing approaches either focus on restrictive classes of risk measures or rely on access to a simulator, limiting their applicability in fully online settings. In this work, we propose computationally efficient online learning algorithms for policy evaluation in Markov decision processes (MDPs) with dynamic utility-based shortfall risk (UBSR) measures under linear function approximation. Specifically, we introduce the UBSR-TD algorithm, establish conditions under which it converges almost surely, and develop several variants designed to accelerate convergence. Our formulation shows that existing policy evaluation algorithms for risk-neutral MDPs can be readily adapted to dynamic UBSR settings by incorporating a loss function into the temporal-difference error. Numerical experiments support our theoretical findings, and an application to a perishable inventory management problem with shelf-life uncertainty demonstrates the practical effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [121] [Self-Boosting Vision-Language Models with Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23125)
*Shuai Wang,Daoan Zhang,Zhe Tang,Hao Cheng,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NOPD（噪声学生同策略自蒸馏）方法，通过干净输入与带噪输入间的预测差异产生自监督信号，无需外部模型或人工标注即可提升视觉语言模型在多项推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM后训练方法依赖人工标注数据、外部模型蒸馏、人类反馈强化学习或可验证答案，缺乏在无外部监督下自我提升的能力。

Method: NOPD采用同策略自蒸馏框架：模型在干净输入上生成token级软标签作为监督信号，同时在加噪输入上进行学习，利用预测不一致性作为自监督信号来驱动模型改进。

Result: 在五个视觉推理任务上，NOPD匹配甚至超越强化学习方法和外部模型蒸馏；在Geometry3K的2.1K样本上训练后，Qwen2.5-VL-7B验证集提升20个点；在MathVista上实现7.4个点的提升；在三个模型12个基准上均取得改进。

Conclusion: NOPD是一种无需外部监督的通用VLM增强方法，能有效利用模型自身的预测一致性作为学习信号，在多种模型和任务上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Post-training enables vision-language models (VLMs) to understand human instructions and perform various downstream tasks. Current post-training methods usually rely on human-annotated data, distillation from external models, reinforcement learning with human feedback, or verifiable answers. This limits their ability to improve without external supervision. To tackle this, we propose NOPD (Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation), a simple yet effective self-distillation approach that improves VLMs without any external models or ground-truth answers. Our key insight is that prediction discrepancies between clean and corrupted inputs naturally induce a self-supervision signal. In NOPD, the model learns from corrupted inputs while using its own predictions under clean inputs as token-level supervision. We show the effectiveness of NOPD on five visual reasoning tasks; it can match and even outperform reinforcement learning approaches or distillation from external models. Notably, when trained with 2.1K samples from Geometry3K, NOPD improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 20 points on its validation set. It also shows generalization on out-of-distribution test sets and achieves 7.4 point gains on MathVista. Furthermore, we demonstrate that NOPD is a general approach to enhance VLMs, achieving improvements across three models on 12 benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [In-Context Learning as Implicit Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23153)
*Masahiro Kaneko,Timothy Baldwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于分数的上下文学习（score-conditioned ICL）在结构上等价于策略梯度优化（REINFORCE算法），并推导了注意力更新引起的分布偏移上界，建立了ICL与强化学习中KL约束策略优化之间的类比关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有实证发现LLM可以通过迭代地纳入生成样本及其评估分数作为上下文示例来改进输出，但这一现象背后的理论基础尚不明确，缺乏严格的数学解释。

Method: 1) 构造性证明：展示自注意力机制在特定权重矩阵配置下可实现类似于REINFORCE算法的奖励加权聚合；2) 理论分析：在简化隐状态模型下推导了有界注意力更新引起的分布偏移上界；3) 实验验证：在多个LLM上验证模型对分数信息的利用能力和注意力权重与分数的相关性。

Result: 1) 建立了score-conditioned ICL与策略梯度优化的结构对应关系；2) 推导了分布偏移的精确上界，得到与KL约束策略优化相似的信任区域类比；3) 实验表明LLM能有效利用分数信息将输出分布向高分数样本偏移，且注意力权重与示例分数强相关。

Conclusion: 基于分数的上下文学习可以通过自注意力机制实现类似策略梯度的优化效果，这为理解LLM的上下文学习能力提供了理论解释，并揭示了ICL与强化学习之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can iteratively improve their outputs by incorporating generated samples and their corresponding evaluation scores as in-context examples. Despite these empirical findings, the theoretical foundations underlying this phenomenon remain poorly understood. In this paper, we show that score-conditioned In-Context Learning (ICL) admits a structural correspondence to policy gradient optimization. We first provide a constructive proof that self-attention mechanisms can implement reward-weighted aggregation analogous to the REINFORCE algorithm under specific weight matrix configurations, and discuss the relationship between this construction and the behavior of pretrained transformers. The correspondence is directional in hidden-state space and holds exactly only under the stated simplifying conditions; we quantify its strength empirically. Within our simplified hidden-state model, we furthermore derive an exact upper bound on the distribution shift induced by a bounded attention update, yielding a trust-region-like analogy to KL-constrained policy optimization. We validate our theory through extensive experiments across multiple LLMs, demonstrating that LLMs effectively utilize score information to shift output distributions toward high-scoring exemplars, and that attention weights exhibit a strong correlation with example scores.

</details>


### [123] [WorldDiT: A Unified Diffusion Architecture for World and Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23909)
*Sen Wang,R. Gnana Praveen,Bidhan Roy,Marcos Villagra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WorldDiT提出了一种统一的扩散变换器架构，将动作生成与视觉世界建模耦合，无需大型预训练VLM动作骨干，在四个LIBERO模拟套件中实现了参数与成功率之间的帕累托最优，为未来扩展研究提供了亚十亿参数的强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前许多机器人策略依赖大型预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）作为动作骨干来提升控制性能，但这种方法计算成本高昂且依赖大规模预训练。作者旨在探索是否可以在不使用大型预训练VLM的情况下，通过统一架构同时进行动作生成和视觉世界建模，实现同样强的控制性能。

Method: WorldDiT采用统一的扩散变换器（Diffusion Transformer）架构，在训练过程中同时生成连续动作块（action chunks）并预测来自未来相机帧的归一化RGB patch目标。该架构将动作生成与视觉世界建模耦合在单一模型中。

Result: 在四个LIBERO模拟套件上，WorldDiT在报告所有四个套件的方法中，位于总模型参数与平均成功率之间的帕累托前沿上。这意味着它在参数效率与任务成功率之间取得了最优权衡。

Conclusion: WorldDiT证明了无需大型预训练VLM动作骨干也能实现强控制性能，为未来机器人策略的扩展研究提供了一个亚十亿参数规模的强基线，表明视觉世界建模与动作生成的耦合是一种有前景的方向。

Abstract: Many recent robot policies pursue stronger control by using large pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as the action backbone. We introduce WorldDiT, a unified diffusion transformer architecture that couples action generation with visual world modeling and achieves strong performance without a large pretrained VLM action backbone. During training, a single diffusion transformer generates continuous action chunks and predicts normalized RGB patch targets from future camera frames. Across four LIBERO simulation suites, WorldDiT lies on the reported Pareto frontier for total model parameters and mean success among methods reporting all four suites. These results provide a strong sub-billion-parameter baseline for future scaling studies.

</details>


### [124] [Learning Reusable Hybrid Motion Priors for Humanoid Locomotion from Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24083)
*Valerio Belli,Valerio Modugno,Enrico Mingo Hoffman,Fabio Amadio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种将运动模仿技能转化为可重用的混合运动先验（HMP）的三阶段流程：先训练专家策略模仿人体运动，再蒸馏为含残差向量量化码本的冻结架构，最后训练任务策略通过选择离散码本条目解决下游任务。在仿真和真实机器人上验证了速度跟踪、导航等任务，HMP可重用且码本结构具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可以生成鲁棒的人形控制器，但每个新任务通常需要单独设计奖励和训练策略；运动模仿虽然能提供运动能力，但生成的控制器只是参考跟踪器，无法直接作为任务策略使用。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，构建一个可重用的运动先验。

Method: 三阶段流程：(1) 训练专家策略模仿重定向的人体运动捕捉数据；(2) 将专家蒸馏为冻结架构，包含本体感受编码器、残差向量量化码本和动作解码器；(3) 训练任务级策略通过选择离散码本条目解决具体任务，同时保持HMP冻结。使用旋转技巧改进码本训练。

Result: 在仿真中成功完成速度跟踪、点目标导航和摔倒恢复速度跟踪任务，并在真实Unitree G1机器人上部署了速度跟踪策略。蒸馏保留专家跟踪行为，HMP可重用。旋转技巧改善了潜在空间组织并减少了摔倒次数。

Conclusion: HMP作为可重用的动作接口，无需重新训练即可用于不同下游任务；码本结构具有可解释性，激活的RVQ阶段数量调控步态模式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can produce robust humanoid controllers, but each new task is typically trained as a separate policy with its own reward design and training process. Motion imitation provides an alternative source of motor competence by training policies to track retargeted human motions, yet the resulting controllers remain reference trackers and are not directly usable as task policies. We propose a three-stage pipeline that turns motion-imitation skills into a reusable hybrid motion prior (HMP) for humanoid locomotion. First, an expert policy is trained to imitate retargeted human motion-capture clips. Second, the expert is distilled into a frozen architecture composed of a proprioceptive encoder, a residual vector-quantized (RVQ) codebook, and an action decoder. Third, task-level policies are trained to solve locomotion tasks by selecting discrete codebook entries while the HMP remains frozen. We evaluate the method on velocity tracking, point-goal navigation, and fall-recovery velocity tracking in simulation, and deploy the velocity-tracking policy on a real Unitree G1 robot. The distillation process preserves the tracking behavior of the expert, while the resulting HMP can be reused without retraining as the action interface for different downstream locomotion policies. The learned HMP reveals an interpretable codebook structure in which the number of active RVQ stages modulates the available gait patterns. We further show that training the codebook with the rotation trick improves latent organization and reduces downstream falls compared with a standard straight-through estimator.

</details>


### [125] [AlloBench: Measuring Online Tool Allocation Capability in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23332)
*Daniel Wang,Andrew Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了LLM代理在固定预算下能否智能地分配工具创建资源：在抽象文本任务中表现接近最优，但在实际代码构建任务中普遍失败，揭示了前沿模型在工具分配能力上的显著边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用工具需要前期成本，智能体应优先创建少量高复用性工具而非大量一次性工具。研究者想知道LLM代理是否具备这种有意识的资源分配行为。

Method: 设计了一个配对基准测试，包含抽象文本形式的分配任务和代码构建任务。测试了Claude Haiku、Claude Opus、GPT-5.4-mini和GPT-5.6 Sol等前沿模型。进一步通过消融实验（不评估脚本、仅评估部分环节）识别各模型的失败模式，并测试了经过策略训练的Qwen模型的泛化能力。

Result: 所有模型在抽象框架中表现接近最优，但无法将能力迁移到脚本编写任务。不同模型的失败模式各异：前三者在脚本不评估时仍失败，GPT-5.6 Sol在不完全评估时保持选择性，仅在完整构建中崩溃。Qwen策略模型在抽象分配中泛化良好，但在脚本分配中无提升。

Conclusion: 在线工具分配是前沿模型的一个重要能力边界，即使最先进的LLM也难以从抽象任务泛化到实际构建场景。

Abstract: Creating a reusable tool is an investment: an agent pays a fixed cost now in exchange for the potential of future reuse. Therefore, a user should prefer an agent that creates a small number of highly reusable tools, rather than many one-offs. We introduce a paired benchmark that tests whether LLM agents exhibit conscious allocation behavior under a fixed budget in two contexts: an abstract text-based formulation and a code-construction task. We find that every frontier model we test---Claude Haiku, Claude Opus, GPT-5.4-mini, and GPT-5.6 Sol---acts near-optimally in the abstract framing but fails to transfer this ability to script-writing. Through further experiments, we identify the particular failure modes for each model. Notably, the first three models fail even when the scripts are not evaluated, while GPT-5.6 Sol stays selective under that weaker manipulation and collapses only at full construction. Furthermore, an open-source Qwen model policy-trained for abstract allocation generalizes this ability across held-out lexical variations, but sees no improvement at script allocation. Together, these results establish online tool allocation as a significant capability boundary, even for modern frontier models.

</details>


### [126] [On the Impossibility of Unbiased and Length-Invariant Policy Optimization with Outcome Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23364)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在标准GRPO设定下，不存在同时满足梯度无偏性和长度不变性的长度加权方案。GRPO近似满足长度不变性但有偏，Dr. GRPO无偏但存在长度偏差，两者处于一个根本性权衡的两端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对GRPO及其改进版本Dr. GRPO中关于长度归一化策略的争议，澄清Dr. GRPO声称的"无偏"优化器说法不完整，揭示两者在长度偏差与梯度无偏性之间存在的根本性权衡。

Method: 通过建立不可能性定理证明在标准结果奖励+GRPO设定下，不存在同时满足梯度无偏性(P1)和长度不变性(P2)的长度加权方案；引入参数化族 f_α(L)=L^{α-1} 统一刻画权衡谱系，其中α=0对应GRPO，α=1对应Dr. GRPO。

Result: GRPO近似满足长度不变性但违反无偏性；Dr. GRPO满足无偏性但违反长度不变性；Dr. GRPO的长度偏差会导致较长轨迹的梯度贡献按长度比线性放大。

Conclusion: GRPO和Dr. GRPO都不是通用的"正确方案"，它们处于一个根本且不可避免的权衡的两个极端，研究者需要根据具体任务需求在无偏性和长度不变性之间做出选择。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is the dominant reinforcement learning algorithm for training reasoning capabilities in large language models, notably adopted by DeepSeek-R1. The recent improvement Dr. GRPO (COLM 2025) identifies the response-level length bias caused by per-trajectory length normalization in GRPO and proposes removing this normalization, claiming the resulting optimizer is "unbiased." We show that this claim is incomplete. Specifically, we establish an impossibility theorem: under the standard outcome reward + GRPO setting, no length-based weighting scheme can simultaneously achieve the following two properties. (P1) Gradient unbiasedness: the gradient estimator is an unbiased estimate of the true policy gradient. (P2) Length invariance: each trajectory's effective contribution to the gradient is independent of its token length. GRPO approximately satisfies P2 but violates P1; Dr. GRPO satisfies P1 but violates P2. We characterize the complete tradeoff spectrum via the parametric family f_alpha(L) = L^{alpha - 1}, where alpha = 0 recovers GRPO, alpha = 1 recovers Dr. GRPO, and provide quantitative analysis showing that Dr. GRPO's length bias can cause longer trajectories to dominate gradient updates by a factor proportional to the length ratio. Our results reveal that neither algorithm is universally "done right"; they occupy opposite ends of a fundamental and unavoidable tradeoff.

</details>


### [127] [Learning to Optimize: Joint Routing and Flow Allocation on Sparse Non-Euclidean Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23467)
*Haomiao Sun,Fang He,Congyuan Ji,Xindi Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双通道图注意力（DCGA）强化学习框架，用于求解稀疏非欧网络上的集成取送货问题，联合优化循环路径、货运流量分配和跨周期服务，在LinerLib基准上实现了秒级推理和最先进解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成取送货问题中循环路径、流量分配与跨周期服务的紧耦合导致了复杂的离散-连续混合决策空间和高度受限的可行域，传统方法难以高效求解。

Method: 提出双通道图注意力（DCGA）端到端强化学习框架，将网络可达性和需求-服务逻辑分离为两个独立的图通道，并通过耦合模拟器的约束感知解码器构建有效路径。

Result: 在LinerLib基准上，DCGA实现秒级推理，在特定规模以上的实例上达到最先进的解质量，且优势随问题规模增大而扩大。稳定性和消融分析验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 这种结构感知的学习方法为现实的路径与流量联合优化提供了高效、低延迟的求解引擎。

Abstract: We study an integrated pickup-and-delivery problem on sparse, non-Euclidean networks that jointly optimizes cyclic routing, cargo flow allocation, and cross-cycle service. The tight coupling of these operational constraints creates a complex discrete-continuous decision space with highly restricted feasible regions. To overcome these computational challenges, we propose Double-Channel Graph Attention (DCGA), an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework. DCGA isolates network reachability and demand-service logic into separate graph channels and constructs valid routes using a simulator-coupled, constraint-informed decoder. Experiments on LinerLib benchmarks demonstrate that DCGA achieves seconds-level inference and delivers state-of-the-art solution quality on instances beyond a specific scale, with its advantage over existing baselines widening significantly as problem size increases. Supported by extensive stability and ablation analyses, our results demonstrate that this structure-aware learning approach provides an effective, low-latency engine for realistic routing-and-flow optimization.

</details>


### [128] [Sparse Gaussian-Mixture-Model Q-Functions via Hadamard Overparametrization for Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23474)
*Minh Vu,Konstantinos Slavakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏高斯混合模型Q函数（S-GMM-QF）的在线离策略策略迭代强化学习框架，通过Hadamard过参数化和黎曼优化实现可解释的稀疏化，在参数效率、泛化能力和可解释性方面优于或媲美深度强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法参数规模庞大、可解释性差，且在低参数场景下泛化能力下降；同时，在线非平稳数据与参数空间的黎曼结构之间存在不匹配问题，分布偏移也需要处理。

Method: 采用Hadamard过参数化构建S-GMM-QF，通过平滑正则化实现可解释的稀疏化；利用经验回放处理分布不匹配；在笛卡尔积黎曼流形上进行在线梯度下降优化，每个高斯分量的参数（均值和协方差）显式编码其在状态-动作空间中的几何角色。

Result: 数值实验表明，S-GMM-QF在参数大幅减少的情况下，性能匹配或超越深度强化学习方法，且每次观测转换的改进速度更快；在低参数场景下，稀疏化深度RL方法性能退化，而S-GMM-QF仍保持强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 参数效率与几何可解释性的结合使得S-GMM-QF在低参数场景下具有显著优势，为强化学习提供了一条兼顾性能与可解释性的新路径。

Abstract: This paper develops an online, off-policy policy-iteration framework for reinforcement learning (RL), based on sparse Gaussian-mixture-model Q-functions (S-GMM-QFs). The framework reconciles streaming, non-stationary data with the Riemannian structure of the parameter space while handling distributional mismatch through experience replay. S-GMM-QFs are introduced via Hadamard overparametrization, enabling interpretable sparsification through smooth regularization that facilitates Riemannian-based optimization. Overparametrization allows the framework to adaptively identify meaningful components from a large initial pool, yielding sparse models where interpretability emerges naturally from geometry: each component's parameters (means and covariances) explicitly encode its geometric role in the ambient state-action space. These geometric roles are learned through online gradient descent on a smooth objective over a (Cartesian-product) Riemannian manifold. Numerical tests demonstrate that S-GMM-QFs match or exceed deep RL methods while using substantially fewer parameters and achieving faster improvement per observed transition. Notably, parameter efficiency and interpretability combine to maintain strong generalization in low-parameter regimes where sparsified deep RL approaches degrade.

</details>


### [129] [Learning Sampling Parameters for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23488)
*Arisrei Lim,Yossi Gandelsman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeSAMP是一个框架，通过强化学习训练大语言模型，为文本到图像扩散模型生成提示条件化的、随时间步变化的采样参数（如引导尺度、噪声调度等），在Flux.1和SD 3.5上相比基线方法实现了最高68.12%（人类偏好分数）和73.37%（VLM评判）的胜率，用户研究中胜率达59.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在推理时使用固定的采样参数（如引导尺度、噪声调度），但不同提示和生成阶段需要不同的参数值，手动选择这些参数效率低下且次优。

Method: 将参数选择建模为强化学习问题：训练一个大语言模型，根据用户提示生成采样参数的时间步调度；使用人类偏好模型和VLM-as-a-judge的奖励信号进行优化。

Result: 在Flux.1 [dev]和Stable Diffusion 3.5上，LeSAMP比基线方法在人类偏好分数上胜率达68.12%，VLM评判胜率达73.37%，用户研究中胜率达59.46%。

Conclusion: 学习采样参数策略为改进扩散模型输出提供了一种与现有后训练方法互补的有效途径。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models expose many inference-time sampling parameters, including prompts, negative prompts, classifier-free guidance scales, and noise schedules. These parameters are typically manually chosen once and then held fixed across prompts and denoising timesteps, even though different prompts and stages of generation can benefit from different parameter values. We introduce LeSAMP, a framework for learning prompt-conditioned, timestep-varying sampling parameters. We formulate parameter selection as a reinforcement learning problem: Given a user prompt, a large language model is trained to emit schedules for the chosen sampling parameters. We optimize our model using rewards from human preference models and VLM-as-a-judge. We evaluate our model on Flux.1 [dev] and Stable Diffusion 3.5, and find that compared to baselines, LeSAMP has a win rate of up to 68.12% using human preference scores and 73.37% using VLM-as-a-judge. These gains are validated in a user study where we achieve win rates of up to 59.46% over previous baselines. Our results suggest that learned sampling-parameter policies provide a complementary approach to existing post-training methods for improving diffusion model outputs.

</details>


### [130] [Optimal Reward Shaping: Autonomous Car Parking Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23617)
*Emre Özkaya,Nicolas R. Gauger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数化奖励塑形框架，通过覆盖门控对齐反馈、驱动方向切换正则化和对齐回合终止机制，解决非完整约束下模型无关强化学习中的奖励函数设计难题。研究表明环境奖励参数与算法超参数深度耦合，需通过基于代理的贝叶斯优化进行联合元优化。经联合优化的DQN智能体在自主平行泊车任务中显著优于未校准基线，成功率和轨迹平滑度均大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完整约束下模型无关强化学习的奖励函数设计存在严重局部极小值问题（如策略瘫痪或过度保守避障），传统方法难以有效解决。

Method: 提出参数化奖励塑形框架，包含覆盖门控对齐反馈、驱动方向切换正则化、对齐回合终止机制三大组件；采用基于代理的贝叶斯优化对环境奖励参数和算法超参数进行联合元优化。

Result: 联合优化的DQN智能体在自主平行泊车任务中成功率和轨迹平滑度均显著优于未校准基线，有效解决了特征性控制失败模式。

Conclusion: 环境奖励参数与算法超参数存在深度耦合关系，联合元优化是实现稳定收敛的关键；所提框架为非完整约束下的强化学习任务提供了有效的奖励塑形方案。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions for model-free reinforcement learning under non-holonomic constraints remains a persistent challenge, often resulting in severe local minima such as policy paralysis or over-conservative hazard avoidance. In this work, we present a parameterized reward shaping framework featuring coverage-gated alignment feedback, drive-direction switch regularization, and an aligned episode termination mechanism evaluated on an autonomous parallel parking task. Crucially, we show that environmental reward parameters and algorithmic hyperparameters are deeply co-dependent, requiring joint meta-optimization to achieve stable convergence. By employing surrogate-based Bayesian optimization, our co-optimized Deep Q-Network (DQN) agent resolves characteristic control failure modes, significantly outperforming uncalibrated baselines across both success rate and trajectory smoothness.

</details>


### [131] [Breaking the Total Variance Barrier: Sharp Sample Complexity for Linear Heteroscedastic Bandits with Fixed Action Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23679)
*Heyang Zhao,Tianyuan Jin,Weixin Wang,Vincent Y. F. Tan,Pan Xu,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对异方差噪声下的随机线性Bandit问题，提出了一种方差感知的主动探索算法VAEE，实现了接近调和均值的简单遗憾界，突破了现有方法中$\sqrt{\Lambda}$依赖的瓶颈，并给出了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异方差线性Bandit方法使用累积方差$\Lambda$刻画统计复杂度，但$\Lambda$在噪声半数为零时仍保持不变，说明其依赖不是最优的。本文旨在打破$\sqrt{\Lambda}$的依赖瓶颈，实现更紧的遗憾界。

Method: 针对大动作集，提出VAEE算法，在未被淘汰的候选动作集中主动探索能最大化信息增益的动作；针对有限动作集，提出基于G-optimal设计的方差感知变体进行探索。

Result: VAEE实现了接近调和均值的简单遗憾率；有限动作集下得到对$d$更尖锐的依赖；建立了几乎匹配的下界，表明调和均值依赖率不可避免。

Conclusion: 这是首个突破异方差随机线性Bandit中$\sqrt{\Lambda}$屏障的工作，证明了调和均值依赖率是最优的。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed increasing interests in tackling heteroscedastic noise in bandits and reinforcement learning. In these works, the cumulative variance of the noise $Λ= \sum_{t=1}^T σ_t^2$, where $σ_t^2$ is the variance of the noise at round $t$, is used to characterize the statistical complexity of the problem, yielding \emph{simple regret} bounds of order $\tilde{\cal{O}}(d \sqrt{Λ/ T^2})$ for $d$-dimensional linear bandits with heteroscedastic noise. However, with a closer look, $Λ$ remains the same order even if the noise is close to zero at half of the rounds, which indicates that the $Λ$-dependence is not optimal. In this paper, we revisit the stochastic linear bandit problem with heteroscedastic noise, where the action set is prefixed throughout the learning process. We propose a novel variance-adaptive algorithm \texttt{VAEE} (Variance-Aware Exploration with Elimination) for large action set, which actively explores actions that maximizes the information gain among a candidate set of actions that are not eliminated. With the active-exploration strategy, we show that \texttt{VAEE} achieves a \emph{simple regret} with a nearly \emph{harmonic-mean} dependent rate. For finitely many actions, we propose a variance-aware variant of G-optimal design based exploration, which achieves a simple regret with sharper dependence on $d$. We also establish a nearly matching lower bound for the fixed action set setting indicating that \emph{harmonic-mean} dependent rate is unavoidable. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work that breaks the $\sqrtΛ$ barrier for stochastic linear bandits with heteroscedastic noise.

</details>


### [132] [Constrained Reinforcement Learning Using Successor Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24057)
*Michael Girstl,Alexander Mattick,Christopher Mutschler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeDSR方法，通过可学习的权重矩阵解耦价值函数中的动态、奖励和成本，使策略能在成本结构变化时快速重新训练，无需重新训练整个网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实强化学习需要将安全约束融入策略，但现有方法难以适应成本函数的变化（如域迁移或障碍物移动），缺乏灵活性。

Method: SafeDSR扩展了Deep Successor Representation，引入单个可学习权重矩阵来解耦价值函数中的动态、奖励和成本，当成本结构变化时，只需以监督方式更新该矩阵而非重新训练整个网络。

Result: 在可配置的二维导航环境中，SafeDSR在简单导航任务上表现具有竞争力，同时在灵活性上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SafeDSR通过解耦成本与价值函数的方式，实现了对成本结构变化的快速适应，为约束强化学习在动态现实环境中的应用提供了更灵活的方案。

Abstract: Real-world Reinforcement Learning depends on the ability to formulate safety constraints into a policy. A common way to model such constraints is to introduce an additional cost signal in the Markov Decision Process, which notifies the agent of unwanted behavior independently of the reward signal. Unfortunately, current methods are hard to adapt to changes in the cost function introduced by, e.g., domain shift or obstacles moving over time. The lack of adaptability means that policies are too unflexible to deal with complex real-world conditions. We propose the Safe Deep Successor Representation (SafeDSR), a novel method that allows quick retraining of policies towards new cost structures. SafeDSR extends the Deep Successor Representation (Kulkarni et al., 2016) to Constrained Reinforcement Learning by introducing a single learnable weight matrix to decouple the learned value function across dynamics, rewards, and costs. This matrix can be updated in a supervised manner instead of having to adapt the whole network if the cost structure of the environment changes. We demonstrate this ability in a freely configurable two-dimensional navigation environment and show that our method is competitive on a simple navigation task while being considerably more flexible

</details>


### [133] [ACRL: Adaptive Control of Training-Inference Discrepancy for Stable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24062)
*Wenwu Fan,Qihong Lin,Zhijie Xia,Zhuo Zheng,Sihao Wang,Qiang Chen,Liangsheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型强化学习训练中由于训练-推理差异（架构分离+低精度量化）导致的不稳定性问题，提出自适应控制RL（ACRL）方法，通过动态维持训练-推理差异在合理范围内来稳定训练，同时增加策略熵以提升探索与准确率，在FP8量化下达到BF16基线水平并优于重要性采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的RL训练存在训练-推理差异（架构分离与低精度量化），导致训练不稳定，亟需自适应控制该差异的方法。

Method: 提出ACRL（Adaptive Control Reinforcement Learning），自适应地将训练-推理差异控制在合理范围内，以稳定RL训练。

Result: 在FP8量化推理下，ACRL稳定维持训练-推理差异在合理范围，稳定了RL训练；准确率匹配BF16基线，且优于重要性采样（IS）修复方法。

Conclusion: ACRL能有效控制训练-推理差异，稳定RL训练，同时通过增加策略熵提升探索与精度，是一种优于传统修复方法的稳定训练方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) training for Large Language Models (LLMs) often suffers from instability due to the discrepancy between training and inference. This training-inference discrepancy stems from two primary factors: an architectural separation between training and inference engines, and the use of low-precision quantization in inference versus higher-precision computation in training. To address training instability issues caused by high training-inference discrepancy, we present the principles and methods for its adaptive control. We propose Adaptive Control Reinforcement Learning (ACRL), which adaptively maintains the training-inference discrepancy within a reasonable range to ensure stable RL training. Beyond stabilization, ACRL inherently increases policy entropy, thereby enhancing exploration and improving accuracy. The experimental results show that when the inference engine utilizes FP8 quantization, ACRL consistently maintains the training-inference discrepancy within a reasonable range and stabilizes RL training. Furthermore, ACRL not only matches the accuracy of the BF16 baseline but also outperforms importance sampling (IS) fixes.

</details>


### [134] [Context Is King: How In-Context Specification Shapes the Geometry of Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24425)
*Elad David,Max Fomin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大型语言模型中的几何结构主要由上下文指定而非预训练先验决定：同一组词元可在不同上下文指令下形成循环或树形拓扑，且这种结构在能力更强的模型中会主导先验知识，并通过激活修补证明其被因果使用；但该机制的清晰运作仅出现在较大规模模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统认知——即LLM中的结构化概念（如星期、月份）存储于固定的世界模型几何中；探索上下文规范能否覆盖并重新定义模型实际使用的几何拓扑结构。

Method: 通过对比预训练先验（如星期循环）与上下文声明性规则（如指定任意无意义词元构成循环或树结构），使用表征相似性分析、激活修补（因果干预）等方法，在Gemma和Qwen系列模型上测试几何结构的使用情况。

Result: 上下文指定的几何结构在能力强的模型中主导先验（表征相似度0.6-0.9，先验近零）；激活修补证明结构被因果使用；小模型也能形成粗略映射，但仅在较大模型（Gemma-31B, Qwen-27B）中实现清晰主导和因果交叉。

Conclusion: LLM实际使用的几何结构由上下文决定，而非固定先验；该机制具有规模依赖性，大模型与小模型在机制存在性上可能有质的不同。

Abstract: Large language models place structured concepts on geometrically faithful manifolds: weekdays lie on a circle, months on another, usually taken to be a fixed world-model the network stores and looks up. We show that context is king: the structure a model actually uses is set by the in-context specification. A declarative rule fixes not only which relations the geometry encodes but its topology type: the same tokens form a cycle or a branching tree on command, built even on arbitrary, meaning-free tokens with no prior to inherit, which a relabeled stored shape cannot do. When the specification conflicts with a strong pretrained prior, the context-set geometry dominates it in capable models, read from the same activations (representational similarity 0.6--0.9 to the imposed structure versus near-zero to the prior), across the priors we test and both families we study (Gemma, Qwen). Activation patching shows the map is causally used, not a probe correlate: swapping one entity's activation for another's makes the model answer with the other entity's successor under the imposed order. A rough map forms readily, present even in small and base models; what scale gates is using it cleanly: clean dominance and the causal crossover emerge only in the larger models (up to Gemma-31B and Qwen-27B) and weaken or reverse below, so a mechanism present in a large model can be absent in a smaller one of the same family. Whether the model builds this geometry anew or reconfigures a stored one we leave open; operationally, the geometry it uses is the one the context specifies.

</details>


### [135] [FlowCTS: On-policy Continuous Trajectory Supervision of Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24522)
*Kaiyang Ye,Yuan Ge,Junxiang Zhang,Bei Li,Ziming Zhu,Haishu Zhao,Xiaoqian Liu,Chenglong Wang,Jingbo Zhu,Zhengtao Yu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowCTS方法，通过连续轨迹监督解决流模型在知识蒸馏中的稀疏奖励和曝光偏差问题。该方法利用速度场积分关系推导出时间加权速度匹配上界，并离散化为实用目标。在多个基准测试上，FlowCTS-OPD优于传统KL蒸馏和混合奖励RL基线，并揭示了KL-OPD中存在的时间监督不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）在解决大语言模型后训练中的稀疏奖励和曝光偏差问题方面效果显著，但其在流模型上的扩展尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Flow Continuous Trajectory Supervision (FlowCTS)，该方法匹配从同一学生访问状态初始化的后续学生轨迹和参考轨迹。利用轨迹与速度场之间的积分关系，推导出时间加权速度匹配上界，并将其离散化为以监督步数为参数的实用目标函数。

Result: 单状态FlowCTS-OPD在GenEval上从0.90提升至0.93，OCR从0.90提升至0.92，PickScore从22.75提升至23.06，且在所有目标指标上均优于混合奖励RL基线。进一步分析揭示了传统KL-OPD中由辅助SDE转移核引起的时间监督不匹配问题。在非同策略设置下，FlowCTS也始终优于原始SFT。

Conclusion: FlowCTS有效解决了流模型蒸馏中的关键问题，性能优于多种基线方法。增加监督步数在更丰富的轨迹信息与更大的优化难度之间存在权衡。

Abstract: While on-policy distillation (OPD) effectively addresses sparse rewards and exposure bias in large language model post-training, its extension to flow models remains underexplored. To this end, we propose Flow Continuous Trajectory Supervision (FlowCTS), which matches subsequent student and reference trajectories initialized from the same student-visited state. Using the integral relation between trajectories and velocity fields, we derive a temporally weighted velocity-matching upper bound and discretize it into practical objectives parameterized by the number of supervision steps. Under a multi-reference setup, single-state FlowCTS-OPD outperforms vanilla KL-based OPD with faster convergence. FlowCTS-OPD improves GenEval from 0.90 to 0.93, OCR from 0.90 to 0.92, and PickScore from 22.75 to 23.06, while outperforming a mixed-reward RL baseline across all target metrics. Further analysis reveals a clear temporal supervision mismatch in vanilla KL-based OPD arising from its auxiliary SDE transition kernels. Beyond on-policy setting,FlowCTS also consistently outperforms vanilla SFT , particularly on OCR, while increasing supervision steps exhibit a trade-off between richer trajectory information and greater optimization difficulty.

</details>


### [136] [Evaluating Fuzz Testing for Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24577)
*Zhibin Kang,Hanmo You,Dong Wang,Haiming Zheng,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对强化学习（RL）智能体的模糊测试方法进行了全面实证研究，从有效性、多样性、效率、实用性四个维度系统评估了五种最先进方法，揭示了不同方法在崩溃发现、行为多样性、鲁棒性提升和安全监控方面的优缺点，并提出了结合互补策略和多层次多样性分析等实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL智能体在安全关键领域的部署日益增多，其意外行为可能导致严重后果。虽然已有大量RL模糊测试方法，但现有研究在评估设置、基线和指标上存在差异，难以可靠比较各方法的相对有效性和实际可用性。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过统一配置下的系统评估提供可靠的实证结论。

Method: 选取五种最先进RL模糊测试方法（包括MDPFuzz、SeqDivFuzz等）与随机测试作为基线，在三个复杂度递增的环境（MountainCar、BipedalWalker、CARLA）中，从有效性（崩溃发现）、多样性（崩溃行为多样性）、效率（吞吐量）和实用性（鲁棒性提升与安全监控）四个互补视角进行系统评估。

Result: 吞吐量导向的方法（如MDPFuzz）在崩溃发现的有效性和效率上表现最优；明确鼓励探索的方法（如SeqDivFuzz）在发现多样化崩溃行为方面表现突出；模糊测试生成的崩溃数据能够有效提升智能体鲁棒性，并实现具有跨方法泛化能力的准确安全监控。

Conclusion: 结合互补的模糊测试策略、采用多层次多样性分析能够实现更全面和实用的RL测试；为研究者和从业者提供了可操作的指导建议。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains such as robotics, autonomous driving, and drone control, where unexpected behaviors may lead to severe real-world consequences. Fuzz testing has recently emerged as a promising method for exploring the vast state spaces of RL agents and exposing crashes. Although numerous RL fuzzing methods have been proposed, existing studies often differ in evaluation settings, baselines, and metrics, making it difficult to draw reliable conclusions about their relative effectiveness and practical usefulness. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive empirical study that systematically evaluates RL fuzzing methods from four complementary perspectives: effectiveness, diversity, efficiency, and practical utility. We benchmark five state-of-the-art methods alongside random testing under unified configurations across three environments of increasing complexity (MountainCar, BipedalWalker, and CARLA), and further assess the downstream usefulness of detected crashes for agent robustness improvement and safety monitoring. Our results reveal several key insights. For instance,throughput-oriented methods like MDPFuzz demonstrate superior effectiveness and efficiency in crash discovery, while methods explicitly designed to encourage exploration like SeqDivFuzz excel at uncovering diverse crash behaviors. We also show that fuzzing-generated crashes can meaningfully improve agent robustness and enable accurate safety monitoring with strong cross-method generalization. Beyond these empirical findings, we distill actionable guidance for both researchers and practitioners, highlighting the benefits of combining complementary fuzzing strategies and adopting multi-level diversity analysis to achieve more comprehensive and practical RL testing.

</details>


### [137] [Explainable Reinforcement Learning via Physics-Aware Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24672)
*Shaker Al-Tamari,Waled Kadour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过策略蒸馏框架，将高性能连续控制DRL代理（TD3）的策略转化为可解释的浅层决策树，在倒立摆基准上实现了与专家教师等效的性能，同时保持了BIBO稳定性，并揭示了从连续控制到离散规则控制转换中产生的高频Bang-Bang驱动和双模态极限环这一根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人学和汽车工程等安全关键领域，深度强化学习的部署常因其深度神经网络的黑箱特性而受阻，缺乏透明度给法规合规性和人机信任带来重大挑战。因此，本文旨在让高性能连续控制DRL系统变得可解释。

Method: 采用策略蒸馏框架，以高性能Twin Delayed DDPG（TD3）代理作为不透明的连续教师模型，通过自定义物理感知特征和"噪声音响回放"（Noisy Oracle Rollouts）生成数据集，将教师策略蒸馏为基于浅层决策树的可解释学生替代模型。

Result: 蒸馏过程实现了与专家教师等效的性能；同时发现从连续控制到离散规则控制转换会引入高频Bang-Bang驱动和稳定的双模态极限环；BIBO稳定性得以维持，并提供了全局和局部可解释性。

Conclusion: 本文论证了利用决策树对高性能连续控制DRL策略进行可解释蒸馏的可行性，在保持系统稳定性的同时实现了透明化，为安全关键自主系统提供了可解释性解决方案。

Abstract: In safety-critical sectors such as robotics and automotive engineering, the deployment of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) is often hindered by the black-box nature of deep neural networks. This lack of transparency poses significant challenges for regulatory compliance and human-agent trust. This paper presents an experimental study aimed at making high-performance continuous control DRL systems interpretable. A policy distillation framework is implemented using the classic Inverted Pendulum benchmark. A high-performance Twin Delayed DDPG (TD3) agent serves as an opaque, continuous teacher model, whose policy is distilled into an interpretable student surrogate based on a shallow Decision Tree. By leveraging a custom physics-aware feature and "Noisy Oracle Rollouts" for dataset generation, the distillation process achieves performance equivalent to the expert teacher. Furthermore, comparative control theory analysis reveals a fundamental trade-off: transitioning from continuous to discrete rule-based control induces high-frequency Bang-Bang actuation and a stable bimodal limit cycle. Simulation results indicate that Bounded-Input Bounded-Output (BIBO) stability is maintained while providing both global and local interpretability for safe autonomous systems.

</details>
